# M11 plan: corpus, golden tests, platform matrix, escaping fuzz

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** The checks that M8-M10 ran by hand from a scratchpad move into the repository and CI: a pinned list of 20 public specs (`corpus.yaml`) that a tool downloads and verifies, a lock of the generated output (`corpus.lock`) that CI compares, runtime decoding tests of every generated client (spec examples and synthetic values through `fromJson`, `toJson`, `fromJson`), the escaping fuzz with its three checks, a semver rule for output changes, the SDK and platform matrix in layers, SARIF checked offline against its schema and GitHub code scanning, and the browser's `Cookie` checked in Chrome (R15.3, R15.4); the generator defects the corpus and the round trips found are fixed, and a discriminator variant is selected by the value it fixes (a user decision).

**Architecture:** A third workspace package, `packages/typed_openapi_tool` (`publish_to: none`), holds every tool, so the generator gains no dependency (the tool brings `args`, `crypto`, `path`, `yaml`; downloads use `dart:io`, archives the system `tar`). It uses the generator's public API only: `TypedOpenApiCli` for `generate`, `ConfigLoader` and `SpecChecker` for the diagnostics and the IR, and `ApiLibrary`, which task 10 exports for the in-process fuzz. `bin/corpus.dart` has `fetch` (task 2), `update` and `check` (task 3; task 4 adds the round trips), `semver` (task 11); `bin/fuzz.dart` is task 10. The first corpus run found a defect of the emitter, fixed first (task 1). Task 5 derives a discriminator value from a variant's constant (TOA457); tasks 6-9 fix the two defects the round trips found, each after a refactor commit that makes room in an allowlisted emitter with byte-identical output. Task 12 writes the workflows, task 13 pins the platform rows, task 14 checks and fixes SARIF, task 15 checks the browser's `Cookie`, task 16 the documents.

**Tech Stack:** Dart `>=3.10.0 <4.0.0`, pure Dart; tests on `package:test`; generated code analyzed with `dart analyze --fatal-infos` under `package:lints/recommended` plus the `flutter_lints` rules in a subprocess; the runtime also in Chrome (`dart test -p chrome`, `--compiler dart2wasm`); workflows validated with `actionlint`.

**Spec:** [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-13-m11-corpus-design.md](../specs/2026-10-13-m11-corpus-design.md), decisions 1-8 (binding). Requirements: R15.3, R15.4 ([requirements.md](../../requirements.md)). Carried over: every roadmap row with the target M11, and the "Parked from the M9/M10 reviews" tables ([roadmap](../../roadmap.md)). Code rules and size limits: [CLAUDE.md](../../../CLAUDE.md), [code-quality-review.md](../../code-quality-review.md). Sources and licenses of the specs: [research/08-test-corpus.md](../../research/08-test-corpus.md).

## Global Constraints

- SDK `>=3.10.0 <4.0.0`; every package pure Dart; primary constructors (3.13) are not used.
- Dart 3.10 is used actively: dot shorthands, null-aware elements, switch expressions and patterns, records, class modifiers. In tests the arguments of `expect(...)` use full names.
- OOP, no top-level functions except `main` in `bin/` and tests (the tool's test helper `clientIr` is a test helper, as the generator's `emitFixture`).
- The generator gets no new dependency; `packages/typed_openapi_tool` is `publish_to: none`, depends on the generator with `any` (the version rises with output changes, decision 7) and uses only its public API (`lib/typed_openapi.dart`).
- The repository keeps only the pins (`corpus.yaml`), never a spec. Every URL is fixed to a commit, a tag or a release; every entry has a SHA-256, a license and an expectation. The company's private spec is never used.
- Tests never touch the network: only `corpus fetch` (and `update`/`check`, which fetch what the cache lacks) downloads. Temp files only through `test/helpers/temp_dir.dart`.
- Deterministic output: `corpus.lock` is JSON with sorted keys; the same specs and generator give the same bytes on any machine (no absolute path in the output or the lock).
- Every spec string is escaped at every sink; the fuzz checks it (decision 5).
- Size limits (CLAUDE.md, "Code quality rules"): a `lib/` file at most 300 lines, a test file 500, a method 40; the ratchet tests of every package pass after every task, the tool package gets its own with empty allowlists. No allowlisted unit grows: room is made by moving code out first, in `refactor(...)` commits that change no generated byte (tasks 6 and 8, checked by `corpus check`); the restriction on `schema_mapper.dart` and `schema_shapes.dart` of M10 is lifted (the other session is over), and M11 does not need them.
- Every change of generated output runs `corpus update` in its own task and writes "Output changes" in the generator's CHANGELOG (tasks 5, 7, 9).
- `dart format lib test bin` (runtime: `lib test`) changes 0 files on a second run in every package; `dart analyze packages` from the root prints `No issues found!`; no `// ignore:`.
- `dart test` and `dart format` run from the package directory; `dart pub get`, `dart analyze packages`, `git apply` and `git` from the root. New files are created from their blocks in full; diffs are applied with `git apply` from the root; `corpus.lock` is written by the step that says so (it is 3.7 MB and not in this plan).
- Tests that need a platform the plan could not run locally (Windows, macOS, SDK 3.10.0) are written with `@TestOn` or run by the CI matrix; they are verified only in CI and kept minimal.
- Every commit is a Conventional Commit and ends with the `Co-Authored-By` line of the model that does the task (the examples use `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`).
- Documents are in English.

## Decisions taken

The spec's decisions are binding. These are the technical choices it leaves to the plan; choices users see are under "Open questions", each with the default this plan implements.

1. **Where the tool lives.** `packages/typed_openapi_tool`, a workspace package that is never published (so `dart analyze packages` and the size ratchet cover it). The generator's dependencies stay as they are; the tool's `HttpDownload` uses `dart:io`.
2. **Specs of several files** (DigitalOcean, Grace, Medusa) come as the GitHub tarball of the pinned commit (`archive`), of which only `root` is kept. Their pin is a `TreeHash` of the kept files (the lines `path` and `sha256` of each file, sorted), so a byte change of the tarball itself does not break the pin.
3. **The corpus runs both `union` modes** (`raw`, the default, and `structural`), as the M9 and M10 runs did; a client is keyed `<mode>/<name>` in the lock. Each mode is one scratch package (`corpus_raw`, `corpus_structural`) with every client in `lib/<name>`, the runtime by path and the analyzer gate's lints.
4. **`corpus.lock`** records per client the SHA-256 of every generated file (without the manifest `.typed_openapi.json`), the hash of the whole output, the diagnostics by code, and the round-trip counts `examples`, `synthetic`, `spec_defects`, `failures`. `check` lists every changed, added or removed file and every code whose count changed.
5. **The decoding tests use the IR.** `ExampleReader` reads `example`/`examples` only at the locations the IR names (a declaration's schema, a JSON response whose type is a declaration), `SyntheticValues` builds values from the IR alone, and `RoundTripProgram` writes a Dart program per client into the scratch package, with a codec per declaration of the IR. No IR export or hidden command is needed: `ClientCheck.ir` is public.
6. **Round-trip failures are counted, not fatal.** A failure is counted in the lock (`failures`) and printed as a `round trip:` line, so `check` fails on a new or a fixed failure through the lock, not on the failure itself. The first run had three failing values of two generator defects; tasks 7 and 9 fix them, and the final lock has 0 for every client.
7. **The fuzz runs in-process.** `ApiLibrary` joins the public exports (task 10) next to `ModelLibrary`; each seed is one config of six clients (three generation configs times two modes) checked twice by `SpecChecker`. Check 2 ("every string literal equals its spec string at run time") evaluates the literals of each sampled output in the Dart VM and requires every string the IR keeps from the spec (property keys, string enum values, discriminator properties, the discriminator values of object variants, parameter names, event names) among their values. A crash of the generator is not caught (CLAUDE.md: never catch `Error`); the run names the seed and stops.
8. **Semver.** `corpus semver --base <rev>` treats a change of `corpus.lock` (once the base has one) or of a golden file as an output change; it then needs an entry under `### Output changes` in the top section of the generator's CHANGELOG and a raised version (minor before 1.0, major after).
9. **CI layers.** Format and analysis run once (ubuntu, stable): the formatter and the lints of 3.10.0 differ from stable's. Tests run everywhere in the matrix.
10. **Discriminator values from constants (task 5, user decision).** A `$ref` variant the `mapping` does not name, whose discriminator property is fixed to one value, is selected by that value; TOA457 (info) says so when it differs from the component name; a `mapping` entry wins; equal values are TOA456.
11. **A non-object variant of a discriminator union** (an array, a string, a number, a boolean, an enum) is picked by its JSON type before the property is read (task 7); the first variant of a JSON type wins.
12. **Optional constants (task 9).** M8 decision 8 ("`toJson` always writes the value") stays for required constants and for models made in code; a decoded model leaves out of `toJson` the optional constants its JSON did not have (`_absent`), so decode, encode, decode is the identity.
13. **SARIF (task 14).** The schema is vendored (OASIS notice beside it) for offline tests; the checks live in the tool's tests, never in the generator; the reporter writes what GitHub code scanning requires, with `defaultFile` (the config) for a result without a file.
14. **The browser's `Cookie` (task 15)** is checked against a loopback server started by `spawnHybridUri`, so no test uses the external network.

## New commands

```sh
cd packages/typed_openapi_tool
dart run bin/corpus.dart fetch [--cache DIR] [name...]   # download and verify
dart run bin/corpus.dart update [--work DIR] [--lock FILE]  # run the corpus, write corpus.lock
dart run bin/corpus.dart check [--work DIR] [--lock FILE]   # run the corpus, compare with corpus.lock
dart run bin/corpus.dart semver --base origin/main      # CHANGELOG and version for output changes
dart run bin/fuzz.dart --seeds 200 [--from N] [--sample-every 12]
```

No allowlisted unit grows; entries go down or away as code moves out (each in a `refactor` commit, generated output byte-identical, all tests green):

| Entry | Before | After | Task | How |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `lib/src/emit/union_emitter.dart` | 416 | 333 | 6 | `DiscriminatorDecoder`, `UnionVariants` (task 1 changes one line and keeps 416) |
| `lib/src/emit/union_emitter.dart#UnionEmitter._byDiscriminator` | 61 | removed | 6 | `DiscriminatorDecoder.write`, `_cases`, `_otherwise` |
| `lib/src/emit/model_emitter.dart` | 379 | removed (255) | 8 | `ModelConstructors` |
| `lib/src/emit/model_emitter.dart#ModelEmitter._fromJson` | 46 | removed | 8 | `ModelConstructors._fromJson`, `_check`, `_arguments` |
| `lib/src/emit/model_emitter.dart#ModelEmitter.write` | 57 | 46 | 8 | the constructors are written by `ModelConstructors.write` |
| runtime `lib/src/json/json_reader.dart` | 422 | 422 | 9 | `constant` returns a `bool` in the same lines |

The tool package starts with its own `test/quality/size_limits_test.dart` and empty allowlists; its helper `test/helpers/size_limits.dart` is a copy of the generator's, as the runtime's is.

Tasks 1-16 were verified: built and checked on a scratch branch, then replayed from `main` (`c18129b`) using only this plan's blocks (new files created whole, copies with `cp`, diffs applied with `git apply`, the SARIF schema by its download step, `corpus.lock` written by `corpus update` with the scratch cache, and the refactors checked by `corpus check`); after each task `dart analyze packages` is clean, a second `dart format` changes 0 files in every package, the size ratchets pass and `dart test` in every package is green with the counts given in the task; the final tree equals the verified one, and the replayed `corpus.lock` is byte-identical to the scratch one after every task that writes it. The runtime also passes in Chrome on both compilers (`+280`). Before M11: 1292 generator tests and 284 runtime tests (VM).

### Task 1: Fix from the corpus: a nullable list variant of a union is bound before it is encoded

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/type_codec.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/union_emitter.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/fixtures/nullable_variants.yaml`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/generated_code_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/union_nullable_variant_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: M8 `TypeCodec.encodeNullable`, `UnionEmitter._toJson`; the analyzer gate of `test/emit/generated_code_test.dart`.
- Produces: `String TypeCodec.encodeField(DartType type, String field)`: the encoding of a field that may be null, with a pattern (`switch (field) {final v? => ..., null => null}`) where `field == null ? ...` would not promote it; the form `field?.toJson()` and identity encodings stay as they are.

The first corpus run with the five R15.3 specs (before the corpus tool existed, with the M10 scripts) generated GitHub's REST description into code with 2 analyzer errors (`unchecked_use_of_nullable_value`): the 422 error of `pull-request-stacks` is a `oneOf` whose every branch is `nullable: true` (OpenAPI 3.0), so each union variant's type is nullable, and the variant of the array branch wrote `Object? toJson() => value == null ? null : [for (final e0 in value) ...]`, where `value` is a field that Dart does not promote. The model emitter already binds a nullable field with a pattern (M8); the union variant now does the same, through `TypeCodec.encodeField`. Only the shape that did not compile changes; `union_emitter.dart` stays at 416 lines. The fixture `nullable_variants.yaml` joins the analyzer gate.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/fixtures/nullable_variants.yaml`:

````yaml
openapi: 3.0.3
info: {title: Nullable variants, version: "1"}
paths:
  /values:
    get:
      operationId: getValue
      responses:
        '200':
          description: ok
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Value'}
components:
  schemas:
    # GitHub's pull-request-stacks 422 error: every branch is nullable, so
    # each variant's type is nullable, a list among them.
    Value:
      oneOf:
        - {type: string, nullable: true}
        - {type: integer, nullable: true}
        - type: array
          nullable: true
          items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Item'}
        - type: object
          nullable: true
          additionalProperties: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Item'}
    Item:
      type: object
      properties:
        id: {type: string}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/union_nullable_variant_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/config/generation_config.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/overrides/override_set.dart';

import '../helpers/emit_fixture.dart';

/// The `toJson` line of the class [name] in the files [files].
String toJsonOf(Map<String, String> files, String name) {
  final source = files.values.join('\n');
  final start = source.indexOf('class $name ');
  if (start < 0) throw StateError('no class $name');
  final end = source.indexOf('Object? toJson()', start);
  return source.substring(end, source.indexOf('\n', end));
}

void main() {
  for (final mode in UnionMode.values) {
    test('a nullable variant binds its value before it encodes it ($mode)', () {
      final files = emitFixture(
        'nullable_variants.yaml',
        generation: GenerationConfig(union: mode),
      );
      expect(
        toJsonOf(files, 'ValueList'),
        'Object? toJson() => switch (value) {final v? => '
        '[for (final e0 in v) e0.toJson()], null => null};',
      );
      expect(
        toJsonOf(files, 'ValueMap'),
        'Object? toJson() => value?.map((k0, v0) => MapEntry(k0, v0.toJson()));',
      );
      expect(toJsonOf(files, 'ValueString'), 'Object? toJson() => value;');
    });
  }
}
````

Run from `packages/typed_openapi`: `dart test test/emit/union_nullable_variant_test.dart`.

### Task 2: The tool package, `corpus.yaml` and `corpus fetch`

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/bin/corpus.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/corpus.yaml`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/archive_extractor.dart`

- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/corpus_exception.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/corpus_list_reader.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/corpus_spec.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/expectation.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/fetch_command.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/http_download.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/spec_cache.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/spec_source.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/tree_hash.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/pubspec.yaml`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/corpus/corpus_list_reader_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/corpus/corpus_yaml_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/corpus/spec_cache_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/helpers/size_limits.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/helpers/temp_dir.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/quality/size_limits_test.dart`
- Modify: `pubspec.yaml`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the generator's public API only (nothing yet); `package:yaml`, `package:crypto`, `package:args`, `package:path`; the system `tar`.
- Produces: `CorpusSpec`, `SpecSource` (`FileSource`, `ArchiveSource`), `Expectation` (`Generates`, `CheckErrors`), `CorpusListReader.read`, `SpecCache.fetch`/`hashOf`, `TreeHash.of`/`files`/`combine`, `CorpusException`, `Download`, `HttpDownload`, `ArchiveExtractor.extract`, `CorpusCli`, `FetchCommand.addCorpusOptions`/`specsOf` (signatures in "Interfaces between tasks"); `corpus.yaml` with 20 entries.

Spec decisions 1 and 6. `corpus.yaml` pins the five R15.3 specs (Petstore 3 at the `swagger-petstore-v3-1.0.28` tag, Stripe, GitHub's 2022-11-28 description, OpenAI, and the core group of Kubernetes v1.37.1), the 14 specs of M8-M10 at the commits of `research/08-test-corpus.md`, and three code-first specs of open projects: Immich (NestJS), springdoc's Spring Data REST test result (`app17.json`) and Swashbuckle's .NET 10 test site; FastAPI is covered by airflow (and NestJS also by tambo, springdoc by efgs and artemis, Swashbuckle by jellyfin). Each hash was computed from a download during planning. artemis expects `{check-errors: [TOA009]}` (its `!!timestamp` examples). Jellyfin's file is versioned (`12.2`) rather than at a commit, and its license is not stated: it is downloaded, never copied. `fetch` downloads what the cache (`.dart_tool/corpus/specs`) lacks and fails on any hash that differs from its pin, keeping nothing of that download. The tests use a fake download and a `.tar.gz` built with `tar` in a temp directory: they never touch the network.

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/corpus/corpus_list_reader_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/corpus/corpus_list_reader.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/corpus/expectation.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/corpus/spec_source.dart';

const _hash =
    '0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef';

void main() {
  const reader = CorpusListReader();

  test('reads a file entry and an archive entry in file order', () {
    final specs = reader.read('''
specs:
  pets:
    url: https://example.com/abc/openapi.yaml
    sha256: $_hash
    license: MIT
    expect: generate
  shop:
    archive: https://example.com/shop.tar.gz
    root: specs/store
    entry: openapi.yaml
    sha256: $_hash
    license: Apache-2.0
    expect: {check-errors: [TOA009, TOA001]}
''');
    expect([for (final s in specs) s.name], ['pets', 'shop']);
    final pets = specs.first;
    expect(pets.source, isA<FileSource>());
    expect(pets.source.entry, 'openapi.yaml');
    expect(pets.license, 'MIT');
    expect(pets.expectation, isA<Generates>());
    final shop = specs.last.source as ArchiveSource;
    expect(shop.root, 'specs/store');
    expect(shop.entry, 'openapi.yaml');
    expect((specs.last.expectation as CheckErrors).codes, [
      'TOA001',
      'TOA009',
    ]);
  });

  test('an entry without a lowercase sha256 is a FormatException', () {
    expect(
      () => reader.read('''
specs:
  pets: {url: https://example.com/a.yaml, sha256: ABC, license: MIT, expect: generate}
'''),
      throwsA(
        isA<FormatException>().having(
          (e) => e.message,
          'message',
          contains('pets has no lowercase sha256'),
        ),
      ),
    );
  });

  test('an unknown key or expectation is a FormatException', () {
    expect(
      () => reader.read('''
specs:
  pets: {url: u, sha256: $_hash, license: MIT, expect: generate, mode: x}
'''),
      throwsA(isA<FormatException>()),
    );
    expect(
      () => reader.read('''
specs:
  pets: {url: u, sha256: $_hash, license: MIT, expect: compile}
'''),
      throwsA(isA<FormatException>()),
    );
  });
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/corpus/corpus_yaml_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/corpus/corpus_list_reader.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/corpus/spec_source.dart';

void main() {
  final specs = const CorpusListReader().read(
    File('corpus.yaml').readAsStringSync(),
  );

  test('the corpus has the five specs R15.3 names', () {
    expect(
      [for (final s in specs) s.name],
      containsAll(['petstore', 'stripe', 'github', 'openai', 'kubernetes']),
    );
  });

  test('every URL is fixed to a commit, a tag or a versioned file', () {
    final pinned = RegExp(
      r'/[0-9a-f]{40}/|/tar\.gz/[0-9a-f]{40}$|/releases/download/v[\d.]+/|'
      r'-\d+\.\d+\.json$',
    );
    for (final spec in specs) {
      expect('${spec.source.url}', matches(pinned), reason: spec.name);
    }
  });

  test('an archive keeps a directory and names a file in it', () {
    for (final spec in specs) {
      if (spec.source case ArchiveSource(:final root, :final entry)) {
        expect(root, isNot(startsWith('/')), reason: spec.name);
        expect(entry, isNot(contains('..')), reason: spec.name);
      }
    }
  });
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/corpus/spec_cache_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:crypto/crypto.dart';
import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/corpus/corpus_exception.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/corpus/corpus_spec.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/corpus/expectation.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/corpus/spec_cache.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/corpus/spec_source.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/corpus/tree_hash.dart';

import '../helpers/temp_dir.dart';

const _text = 'openapi: 3.1.0\n';

/// An entry for [source] pinned to [sha256].
CorpusSpec spec(SpecSource source, String sha256) => CorpusSpec(
  name: 'pets',
  source: source,
  sha256: sha256,
  license: 'MIT',
  expectation: const Generates(),
);

void main() {
  final file = FileSource(Uri.parse('https://example.com/x/openapi.yaml'));
  final pinned = sha256.convert(utf8.encode(_text)).toString();

  test('downloads a missing file once and serves it from the cache', () async {
    final temp = TempDir();
    var downloads = 0;
    final cache = SpecCache(temp.path, (url) async {
      downloads++;
      return utf8.encode(_text);
    });
    final path = await cache.fetch(spec(file, pinned));
    expect(path, p.join(temp.path, 'pets', 'openapi.yaml'));
    expect(File(path).readAsStringSync(), _text);
    await cache.fetch(spec(file, pinned));
    expect(downloads, 1);
  });

  test('a download with another hash is an error and is not kept', () async {
    final temp = TempDir();
    final cache = SpecCache(temp.path, (url) async => utf8.encode('changed'));
    await expectLater(
      cache.fetch(spec(file, pinned)),
      throwsA(
        isA<CorpusException>().having(
          (e) => e.message,
          'message',
          allOf(contains('corpus.yaml pins $pinned'), contains('pets: https')),
        ),
      ),
    );
    expect(Directory(p.join(temp.path, 'pets')).existsSync(), isFalse);
  });

  test('keeps the root of an archive and pins its tree', () async {
    final temp = TempDir()
      ..write('src/repo-abc/specs/store/openapi.yaml', _text)
      ..write('src/repo-abc/specs/store/paths/a.yaml', 'a: 1\n')
      ..write('src/repo-abc/README.md', 'not kept\n');
    final archive = p.join(temp.path, 'repo.tar.gz');
    final tar = await Process.run('tar', [
      '-czf',
      archive,
      '-C',
      p.join(temp.path, 'src'),
      'repo-abc',
    ]);
    expect(tar.exitCode, 0, reason: '${tar.stderr}');
    final expected = const TreeHash().combine({
      'openapi.yaml': pinned,
      'paths/a.yaml': sha256.convert(utf8.encode('a: 1\n')).toString(),
    });
    final source = ArchiveSource(
      Uri.parse('https://example.com/repo.tar.gz'),
      root: 'specs/store',
      entry: 'openapi.yaml',
    );
    final cache = SpecCache(
      p.join(temp.path, 'cache'),
      (url) async => File(archive).readAsBytesSync(),
    );
    final path = await cache.fetch(spec(source, expected));
    expect(path, p.join(temp.path, 'cache', 'pets', 'openapi.yaml'));
    expect(
      File(p.join(temp.path, 'cache', 'pets', 'README.md')).existsSync(),
      isFalse,
    );
  });
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/quality/size_limits_test.dart`:

````dart
/// The size limits of `CLAUDE.md` ("Code quality rules") for the tool
/// package: a file in `lib/` has at most 300 lines, a test file 500, a
/// method 40. The package starts within them, so nothing is allowlisted.
library;

import 'package:test/test.dart';

import '../helpers/size_limits.dart';

void main() {
  final limits = SizeLimits('.');

  test('no file is over its size limit', () {
    final failures = limits.fileFailures(
      allowlist: const {},
      permanent: const {},
    );
    if (failures.isNotEmpty) fail(failures.join('\n'));
  });

  test('no method is over 40 lines', () {
    final failures = limits.methodFailures(allowlist: const {});
    if (failures.isNotEmpty) fail(failures.join('\n'));
  });
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/corpus.yaml`:

````yaml
# The public specs of the corpus (M11, R15.3). The repository keeps only this
# list: `dart run bin/corpus.dart fetch` downloads each spec into the cache
# and checks its SHA-256 (of the file, or of the tree of an archive's
# `root`). Every URL is fixed to a commit, a tag or a release. `expect` is
# `generate` (the client is generated and passes the analyzer) or
# `{check-errors: [...]}` (the client is skipped for exactly these errors of
# the spec). Sources and licenses: docs/research/08-test-corpus.md.
specs:
  # The five R15.3 names.
  petstore:
    url: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/swagger-api/swagger-petstore/62253dc6d6620572a006757ec7e66606ec36bfb9/src/main/resources/openapi.yaml
    sha256: 5f5facd6c634e42977203e6242a98a5e6e9c26aecb484767f888412faf4b27f9
    license: Apache-2.0
    expect: generate
  stripe:
    url: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/stripe/openapi/7a1f5c21117298a85995746eeeedb6207969dc4e/openapi/spec3.json
    sha256: 7cff4cc46d0654101a36a3302f7544640f5f73e4f3cad6f264f0e23c19fa1776
    license: MIT
    expect: generate
  github:
    url: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/rest-api-description/58b1e0c00b39b9e46c24dc4a0ba6a2669c90e9c1/descriptions/api.github.com/api.github.com.2022-11-28.json
    sha256: b7ded4ae746e70e0c196927534cbfd77a4ecf125bb33f0298a9d218a20e3fc57
    license: MIT
    expect: generate
  openai:
    url: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/openai/openai-openapi/e95c0fe615f45a19e8878af923a729948445a6bd/openapi.yaml
    sha256: 8f037f570565278e84c57ef1f1777c1c8ede954da7b428d70ec93dc294781ba7
    license: MIT
    expect: generate
  # The core group (v1) of Kubernetes v1.37.1, OpenAPI 3.0.
  kubernetes:

    license: Apache-2.0

  # The specs of M8-M10.

    sha256: 4ac04303fa3e5f089418b4a7eec96fcecf5c3e56f36b476136f45924373641a4
    license: Apache-2.0
    expect: generate
  # `example: !!timestamp ...` is TOA009, an error of the spec.
  artemis:
    url: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ls1intum/Artemis/e92f350e7892b514b976b05c4f83cfcec12ec54c/config/openapi/openapi.yaml
    sha256: 2894ffdbbb3d8f35615ea9cae971934fa72543be82571446d126d5b0163ffb20
    license: MIT
    expect: {check-errors: [TOA009]}
  digitalocean:
    archive: https://codeload.github.com/digitalocean/openapi/tar.gz/6fda13183a6046c669b591e057b9d7b3a6344829

  discord:
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/archive_extractor.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

import 'corpus_exception.dart';

/// Extracts one directory of a `.tar.gz` archive with the system `tar`
/// (GNU tar on Linux, bsdtar on macOS and Windows).
final class ArchiveExtractor {
  /// Creates the extractor.
  const ArchiveExtractor();

  /// Writes the directory [root] of the archive [bytes], which sits below
  /// the archive's one top directory (as in a GitHub tarball), into
  /// [target]; [target] must not exist.
  Future<void> extract(List<int> bytes, String root, String target) async {
    final temp = Directory.systemTemp.createTempSync('typed_openapi_tar_');
    try {
      final archive = p.join(temp.path, 'archive.tar.gz');
      File(archive).writeAsBytesSync(bytes);
      final top = await _topDirectory(archive);
      final kept = '$top/$root';
      await _tar(['-xzf', archive, '-C', temp.path, kept]);
      Directory(p.dirname(target)).createSync(recursive: true);
      Directory(p.joinAll([temp.path, top, ...root.split('/')])).renameSync(
        target,
      );
    } finally {
      temp.deleteSync(recursive: true);
    }
  }

  static Future<String> _topDirectory(String archive) async {
    final listing = await _tar(['-tzf', archive]);
    final first = listing.split('\n').first.trim();
    if (first.isEmpty) throw const CorpusException('The archive is empty.');
    return first.split('/').first;
  }

  static Future<String> _tar(List<String> arguments) async {
    final result = await Process.run('tar', arguments);
    if (result.exitCode != 0) {
      throw CorpusException('tar ${arguments.first} failed: ${result.stderr}');
    }
    return '${result.stdout}';
  }
}
````

````dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:args/command_runner.dart';

import 'fetch_command.dart';
import 'http_download.dart';
import 'spec_cache.dart';

/// The `corpus` command line of the repository (M11): `fetch`.
final class CorpusCli {
  /// Creates the command line; lines go to [out], failures and usage errors
  /// to [err]; [download] fetches a spec (the network by default).
  CorpusCli({StringSink? out, StringSink? err, Download? download})
    : _err = err ?? stderr,
      _runner = CommandRunner<int>(
        'corpus',
        'The public-spec corpus of typed_openapi.',
      ) {
    _runner.addCommand(
      FetchCommand(out ?? stdout, _err, download ?? const HttpDownload().call),
    );
  }

  final CommandRunner<int> _runner;
  final StringSink _err;

  /// Runs the command line with [args]; returns the exit code.
  Future<int> run(List<String> args) async {
    try {
      return await _runner.run(args) ?? 0;
    } on UsageException catch (e) {
      _err
        ..writeln(e.message)
        ..writeln()
        ..writeln(e.usage);
      return 64;
    }
  }
}
````

````dart
final class CorpusException implements Exception {
  /// Creates the failure with [message].
  const CorpusException(this.message);

  /// What failed, as a sentence for the person who runs the tool.
  final String message;
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/corpus_list_reader.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:yaml/yaml.dart';

import 'corpus_spec.dart';
import 'expectation.dart';
import 'spec_source.dart';

/// Reads `corpus.yaml`: a map `specs` from a name to its entry.
///
/// An entry has `url` (one file) or `archive`, `root` and `entry` (a
/// `.tar.gz` of several files), a `sha256`, a `license` and `expect`:
/// `generate`, or `{check-errors: [TOA...]}`. A malformed file is a
/// [FormatException] that names the entry: the file is ours, not a user's.
final class CorpusListReader {
  /// Creates the reader.
  const CorpusListReader();

  static final _sha256 = RegExp(r'^[0-9a-f]{64}$');
  static final _name = RegExp(r'^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$');
  static const _keys = {
    'url',
    'archive',
    'root',
    'entry',
    'sha256',
    'license',
    'expect',
  };

  /// The entries of the corpus file [text], in the order of the file.
  List<CorpusSpec> read(String text) {
    final root = loadYaml(text);
    if (root is! YamlMap || root['specs'] is! YamlMap) {
      throw const FormatException('corpus.yaml: no "specs" map.');
    }
    return [
      for (final MapEntry(:key, :value) in (root['specs'] as YamlMap).entries)
        _entry('$key', value),
    ];
  }

  CorpusSpec _entry(String name, Object? value) {
    if (!_name.hasMatch(name)) {
      throw FormatException('corpus.yaml: "$name" is not a snake_case name.');
    }
    if (value is! YamlMap) {
      throw FormatException('corpus.yaml: "$name" is not a map.');
    }
    final unknown = value.keys.where((k) => !_keys.contains(k)).toList();
    if (unknown.isNotEmpty) {
      throw FormatException('corpus.yaml: $name has unknown keys: $unknown.');
    }
    final sha256 = _text(name, value, 'sha256');
    if (!_sha256.hasMatch(sha256)) {
      throw FormatException('corpus.yaml: $name has no lowercase sha256.');
    }
    return CorpusSpec(
      name: name,
      source: _source(name, value),
      sha256: sha256,
      license: _text(name, value, 'license'),
      expectation: _expectation(name, value['expect']),
    );
  }

  SpecSource _source(String name, YamlMap value) {
    if (value['url'] != null) {
      return FileSource(Uri.parse(_text(name, value, 'url')));
    }
    return ArchiveSource(
      Uri.parse(_text(name, value, 'archive')),
      root: _text(name, value, 'root'),
      entry: _text(name, value, 'entry'),
    );
  }

  Expectation _expectation(String name, Object? value) => switch (value) {
    'generate' => const Generates(),
    YamlMap(:final length) when length == 1 => switch (value['check-errors']) {
      final YamlList codes when codes.every((c) => c is String) => CheckErrors(
        [for (final c in codes) '$c']..sort(),
      ),
      _ => throw FormatException(
        'corpus.yaml: $name: "check-errors" is not a list of codes.',
      ),
    },
    _ => throw FormatException(
      'corpus.yaml: $name: "expect" is not "generate" or {check-errors: '
      '[...]}.',
    ),
  };

  static String _text(String name, YamlMap value, String key) =>
      switch (value[key]) {
        final String text when text.isNotEmpty => text,
        _ => throw FormatException('corpus.yaml: $name has no "$key".'),
      };
}
````

````dart
import 'expectation.dart';
import 'spec_source.dart';

/// One public spec of the corpus (`corpus.yaml`): where it is downloaded
/// from, the hash that pins it, its license and what generation is expected
/// to do with it.
final class CorpusSpec {
  /// Creates the entry [name].
  const CorpusSpec({
    required this.name,
    required this.source,
    required this.sha256,
    required this.license,
    required this.expectation,
  });

  /// The name of the entry, also the name of its client.
  final String name;

  /// Where the spec is downloaded from.
  final SpecSource source;

  /// The SHA-256 of the file, or of the tree of an archive (`TreeHash`).
  final String sha256;

  /// The SPDX identifier of the spec's license, or a sentence when the
  /// license is not stated.
  final String license;

  /// What generation does with the spec.
  final Expectation expectation;
}
````

````dart
/// What generation is expected to do with a corpus spec.
sealed class Expectation {
  /// Creates the expectation.
  const Expectation();
}

/// The client is generated, and its code passes the analyzer.
final class Generates extends Expectation {
  /// Creates the expectation.
  const Generates();
}

/// The client is skipped for errors of the spec itself: its error codes are
/// exactly [codes] (artemis: TOA009).
final class CheckErrors extends Expectation {
  /// Creates the expectation of the error codes [codes].
  const CheckErrors(this.codes);

  /// The codes of the errors, sorted.
  final List<String> codes;
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/fetch_command.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:args/args.dart';
import 'package:args/command_runner.dart';

import 'corpus_exception.dart';
import 'corpus_list_reader.dart';
import 'corpus_spec.dart';
import 'spec_cache.dart';

/// `corpus fetch`: downloads the specs of `corpus.yaml` that are not in the
/// cache yet and verifies the hash of every one.
final class FetchCommand extends Command<int> {
  /// Creates the command; lines go to [_out], failures to [_err].
  FetchCommand(this._out, this._err, this._download) {
    addCorpusOptions(argParser);
  }

  final StringSink _out;
  final StringSink _err;
  final Download _download;

  @override
  String get name => 'fetch';

  @override
  String get description =>
      'Download the specs of corpus.yaml into the cache and verify them.';

  /// Adds `--corpus` and `--cache`, which every corpus command takes.
  static void addCorpusOptions(ArgParser parser) => parser
    ..addOption(
      'corpus',
      defaultsTo: 'corpus.yaml',
      help: 'The list of pinned specs.',
    )
    ..addOption(
      'cache',
      defaultsTo: '.dart_tool/corpus/specs',
      help: 'Where the downloaded specs are kept.',
    );

  /// The entries of the corpus file of [results], restricted to the names
  /// in its rest arguments when there are any.
  static List<CorpusSpec> specsOf(ArgResults results) {
    final specs = const CorpusListReader().read(
      File(results.option('corpus')!).readAsStringSync(),
    );
    final only = results.rest.toSet();
    return [
      for (final spec in specs)
        if (only.isEmpty || only.contains(spec.name)) spec,
    ];
  }

  @override
  Future<int> run() async {
    final results = argResults!;
    final cache = SpecCache(results.option('cache')!, _download);
    try {
      for (final spec in specsOf(results)) {
        _out.writeln('${spec.name}: ${await cache.fetch(spec)}');
      }
      return 0;
    } on CorpusException catch (e) {
      _err.writeln(e.message);
      return 1;
    }
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/http_download.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:io';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'corpus_exception.dart';

/// Downloads a URL with `dart:io`, following redirects; only `fetch` uses
/// the network, never a test.
final class HttpDownload {
  /// Creates the download.
  const HttpDownload();

  /// The bytes at [url]; a status other than 200 is a [CorpusException].
  Future<List<int>> call(Uri url) async {
    final client = HttpClient();
    try {
      final response = await (await client.getUrl(url)).close();
      if (response.statusCode != HttpStatus.ok) {
        throw CorpusException('GET $url answered ${response.statusCode}.');
      }
      final bytes = BytesBuilder(copy: false);
      await response.forEach(bytes.add);
      return bytes.takeBytes();
    } finally {
      client.close();
    }
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/spec_cache.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:crypto/crypto.dart';
import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

import 'archive_extractor.dart';
import 'corpus_exception.dart';
import 'corpus_spec.dart';
import 'spec_source.dart';
import 'tree_hash.dart';

/// Downloads the bytes at a URL.
typedef Download = Future<List<int>> Function(Uri url);

/// The downloaded corpus specs: `<directory>/<name>/` per entry, verified
/// against the pinned hash on every use.
final class SpecCache {
  /// Creates the cache in [directory]; [download] fetches a missing spec.
  const SpecCache(this.directory, this._download);

  /// The directory of the cache.
  final String directory;

  final Download _download;

  /// The path of the spec file of [spec]: from the cache when its hash is
  /// the pinned one, else downloaded again. A download whose hash differs
  /// from the pin is a [CorpusException], and nothing of it is kept.
  Future<String> fetch(CorpusSpec spec) async {
    final folder = p.join(directory, spec.name);
    final entry = p.joinAll([folder, ...spec.source.entry.split('/')]);
    if (hashOf(spec) == spec.sha256) return entry;
    _delete(folder);
    final bytes = await _download(spec.source.url);
    switch (spec.source) {
      case FileSource():
        File(entry)
          ..createSync(recursive: true)
          ..writeAsBytesSync(bytes);
      case ArchiveSource(:final root):
        await const ArchiveExtractor().extract(bytes, root, folder);
    }
    final actual = hashOf(spec);
    if (actual != spec.sha256) {
      _delete(folder);
      throw CorpusException(
        '${spec.name}: ${spec.source.url} has the SHA-256 $actual; '
        'corpus.yaml pins ${spec.sha256}.',
      );
    }
    return entry;
  }

  /// The hash of the cached files of [spec], or null when there are none.
  String? hashOf(CorpusSpec spec) {
    final folder = p.join(directory, spec.name);
    if (!Directory(folder).existsSync()) return null;
    return switch (spec.source) {
      FileSource(:final entry) => switch (File(p.join(folder, entry))) {
        final file when file.existsSync() =>
          sha256.convert(file.readAsBytesSync()).toString(),
        _ => null,
      },
      ArchiveSource() => const TreeHash().of(folder),
    };
  }

  static void _delete(String folder) {
    final directory = Directory(folder);
    if (directory.existsSync()) directory.deleteSync(recursive: true);
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/spec_source.dart`:

````dart
/// Where a corpus spec is downloaded from.
sealed class SpecSource {
  /// Creates the source at [url].
  const SpecSource(this.url);

  /// The URL, pinned to a commit or a tag.
  final Uri url;

  /// The path of the spec file in the spec's cache directory.
  String get entry;
}

/// A spec that is one file, saved under the last segment of its URL.
final class FileSource extends SpecSource {
  /// Creates the source at [url].
  const FileSource(super.url);

  @override
  String get entry => url.pathSegments.last;
}

/// A spec of several files: a `.tar.gz` archive whose top directory holds
/// [root]; only [root] is kept, and [entry] is the spec file in it.
final class ArchiveSource extends SpecSource {
  /// Creates the source at [url].
  const ArchiveSource(super.url, {required this.root, required this.entry});

  /// The directory of the archive, below its top directory, that is kept.
  final String root;

  @override
  final String entry;
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/tree_hash.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:crypto/crypto.dart';
import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

/// The SHA-256 of a directory tree: of the lines `<path>` and `<sha256>` of
/// every file, sorted by path, with `/` between segments on every system.
///
/// It pins a spec of several files whatever archive brought them: the bytes
/// of a downloaded archive may change while its files do not.
final class TreeHash {
  /// Creates the hash function.
  const TreeHash();

  /// The hash of the files under [directory].
  String of(String directory) => combine(files(directory));

  /// The hash of each file under [directory], by its path relative to it
  /// with `/` separators, sorted by path.
  Map<String, String> files(String directory) {
    final paths = <String, String>{};
    for (final entity in Directory(directory).listSync(recursive: true)) {
      if (entity is! File) continue;
      final relative = p
          .relative(entity.path, from: directory)
          .replaceAll(r'\', '/');
      paths[relative] = sha256.convert(entity.readAsBytesSync()).toString();
    }
    return Map.fromEntries(
      paths.entries.toList()..sort((a, b) => a.key.compareTo(b.key)),
    );
  }

  /// The hash of [files], a map from a path to the hash of its file.
  String combine(Map<String, String> files) {
    final lines = StringBuffer();
    for (final MapEntry(key: path, value: hash)
        in files.entries.toList()..sort((a, b) => a.key.compareTo(b.key))) {
      lines
        ..writeln(path)
        ..writeln(hash);
    }
    return sha256.convert(utf8.encode(lines.toString())).toString();
  }
}
````

### Task 3: `corpus.lock` and `corpus update` / `corpus check`

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/client_lock.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/corpus_cli.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/corpus_lock.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/corpus_package.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/corpus_run.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/generate_run.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/lock_command.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/package_gate.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/corpus/corpus_lock_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/corpus/corpus_run_test.dart`
- Write (by `dart run bin/corpus.dart update`): `packages/typed_openapi_tool/corpus.lock`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: task 2 (`FetchCommand.addCorpusOptions`, `specsOf`, `SpecCache`, `TreeHash`, `Expectation`); the generator's `TypedOpenApiCli`, `ConfigLoader`, `SpecChecker`, `ClientCheck`, `Diagnostic`, `Severity`, `DiagnosticCode.id`.
- Produces: `ClientLock`, `CorpusLock` (`parse`, `format`, `differencesFrom`), `CorpusPackage`, `GenerateRun`, `GenerateResult`, `PackageGate`, `CorpusRun`, `CorpusResult`, `LockCommand.update`/`.check`.

Spec decisions 2 and 4 (the corpus layer). For each `union` mode a fresh scratch package (`<work>/raw`, `<work>/structural`) gets a config with every spec of `corpus.yaml` at its absolute cached path and an absolute output `lib/<name>`. `generate` runs through `TypedOpenApiCli`, `SpecChecker` once more for the diagnostics by code (the CLI's text report is not parsed for them), and a second `generate` must write and delete nothing. Each client must meet its expectation: a `generate` client is not skipped; a `check-errors` client is skipped for exactly its codes. `PackageGate` runs `dart pub get` (offline first), `dart analyze --fatal-infos` and `dart format --output=none --set-exit-if-changed lib`. `update` writes the lock; `check` lists every difference and fails on one. Both fail when a gate fails. The run test uses three tiny specs (one generates, one is `!!timestamp` with the expected TOA009, one expects to generate and does not) and runs the real gate on them.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/corpus/corpus_lock_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/corpus/client_lock.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/corpus/corpus_lock.dart';

void main() {
  const pets = ClientLock(
    files: {'models/pet.dart': 'b', 'api.dart': 'a'},
    diagnostics: {'TOA651': 2, 'TOA201': 1},
  );
  const skipped = ClientLock(files: {}, diagnostics: {'TOA009': 2});

  test('the text has sorted keys whatever the insertion order', () {
    const lock = CorpusLock({'raw/z': skipped, 'raw/pets': pets});
    final text = lock.format();
    expect(text.indexOf('raw/pets'), lessThan(text.indexOf('raw/z')));
    expect(text.indexOf('api.dart'), lessThan(text.indexOf('models/')));
    expect(text.indexOf('TOA201'), lessThan(text.indexOf('TOA651')));
    expect(text, endsWith('}\n'));
    expect(CorpusLock.parse(text).format(), text);
  });

  test('a skipped client records only its diagnostics', () {
    expect(skipped.toJson(), {
      'diagnostics': {'TOA009': 2},
    });
    expect(pets.toJson()['output'], hasLength(64));
  });

  test('lists every changed file, code and client', () {
    const changed = ClientLock(
      files: {'api.dart': 'a2', 'models/cat.dart': 'c'},
      diagnostics: {'TOA651': 3},
    );
    const got = CorpusLock({'raw/pets': changed, 'raw/new': skipped});
    const want = CorpusLock({'raw/pets': pets, 'raw/z': skipped});
    expect(got.differencesFrom(want), [
      'raw/new: new client',
      'raw/pets: changed api.dart',
      'raw/pets: added models/cat.dart',
      'raw/pets: removed models/pet.dart',
      'raw/pets: TOA201 1 -> 0',
      'raw/pets: TOA651 2 -> 3',
      'raw/z: client is gone',
    ]);
    expect(want.differencesFrom(want), isEmpty);
  });
}
````

````dart
@TestOn('vm')
library;

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/corpus/corpus_run.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/corpus/corpus_spec.dart';

import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/corpus/spec_source.dart';

import '../helpers/temp_dir.dart';

/// An entry named [name] with the expectation [expectation].
CorpusSpec entry(String name, Expectation expectation) => CorpusSpec(
  name: name,
  source: FileSource(Uri.parse('https://example.com/$name.yaml')),
  sha256: '',
  license: 'MIT',
  expectation: expectation,

const _pets = r'''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: Pets, version: "1"}
paths:
  /pets:
    get:
      operationId: listPets
      responses:
        "200":
          description: ok
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: {type: array, items: {$ref: "#/components/schemas/Pet"}}
components:
  schemas:
    Pet: {type: object, required: [id], properties: {id: {type: string}}}
''';

const _broken = '''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: Broken, version: "1"}
paths: {}
components:
  schemas:
    When: {type: string, example: !!timestamp 2001-12-14}
''';

void main() {
  test('generates, locks and checks each expectation', () async {
    final temp = TempDir();
    final inputs = {
      'pets': temp.write('specs/pets.yaml', _pets),
      'broken': temp.write('specs/broken.yaml', _broken),
      'also_broken': temp.write('specs/also.yaml', _broken),
    };
    final result =
        await CorpusRun(
          work: p.join(temp.path, 'work'),
          runtime: p.normalize(
            p.absolute(p.join('..', 'typed_openapi_runtime')),
          ),
          modes: const ['raw'],
        ).run([
          entry('pets', const Generates()),
          entry('broken', const CheckErrors(['TOA009'])),
          entry('also_broken', const Generates()),
        ], inputs);
    expect(result.failures, [
      'raw/also_broken: expected to generate, but it was skipped for '
          '[TOA009].',
    ]);
    final pets = result.lock.clients['raw/pets']!;
    expect(pets.files.keys, containsAll(['api.dart', 'models/pet.dart']));
    expect(pets.files.keys, isNot(contains(CorpusRun.manifest)));
    expect(result.lock.clients['raw/broken']!.files, isEmpty);
    expect(result.lock.clients['raw/broken']!.diagnostics['TOA009'], 1);
  }, timeout: const Timeout(Duration(minutes: 3)));
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/client_lock.dart`:

````dart
import 'tree_hash.dart';

/// What `corpus.lock` records of one client of one `union` mode: the hash of
/// each generated file and of the whole output, and the count of its
/// diagnostics by code.
final class ClientLock {
  /// Creates the record; [files] is empty for a client that is skipped.
  const ClientLock({required this.files, required this.diagnostics});

  /// Reads the record from its JSON [json].
  factory ClientLock.fromJson(Map<String, Object?> json) => ClientLock(
    files: {
      for (final MapEntry(:key, :value)
          in ((json['files'] ?? const <String, Object?>{}) as Map).entries)
        '$key': '$value',
    },
    diagnostics: {
      for (final MapEntry(:key, :value) in (json['diagnostics'] as Map).entries)
        '$key': value as int,
    },
  );

  /// The SHA-256 of each generated file, by its path in the output.
  final Map<String, String> files;

  /// The number of diagnostics of the client by code (`TOA201`).
  final Map<String, int> diagnostics;

  /// The hash of the whole output ([TreeHash] of [files]); null when the
  /// client is skipped.
  String? get output => files.isEmpty ? null : const TreeHash().combine(files);

  /// The record as JSON, keys sorted.
  Map<String, Object?> toJson() => {
    'diagnostics': _sorted(diagnostics),
    if (files.isNotEmpty) 'files': _sorted(files),
    'output': ?output,
  };

  static Map<String, T> _sorted<T>(Map<String, T> map) => Map.fromEntries(
    map.entries.toList()..sort((a, b) => a.key.compareTo(b.key)),
  );
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/corpus_lock.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';

import 'client_lock.dart';

/// `corpus.lock`: the generated output of every corpus client, which CI
/// compares with a new run (spec decision 2).
///
/// The file is JSON with sorted keys and two-space indentation, so the same
/// output gives the same bytes and a change is a readable diff. A client is
/// keyed `<union mode>/<name>` (`raw/stripe`).
final class CorpusLock {
  /// Creates the lock of [clients].
  const CorpusLock(this.clients);

  /// Reads the lock from its text [text].
  factory CorpusLock.parse(String text) {
    final json = jsonDecode(text) as Map<String, Object?>;
    if (json['version'] != version) {
      throw FormatException('corpus.lock: version ${json['version']}.');
    }
    return CorpusLock({
      for (final MapEntry(:key, :value)
          in (json['clients'] as Map<String, Object?>).entries)
        key: ClientLock.fromJson(value as Map<String, Object?>),
    });
  }

  /// The version of the file format.
  static const int version = 1;

  /// The record of each client, by `<union mode>/<name>`.
  final Map<String, ClientLock> clients;

  /// The text of the lock file.
  String format() {
    final keys = clients.keys.toList()..sort();
    return '${const JsonEncoder.withIndent('  ').convert({
      'clients': {for (final key in keys) key: clients[key]?.toJson()},
      'version': version,
    })}\n';
  }

  /// The differences from the [expected] lock, one line each: clients that
  /// appeared or are gone, files added, removed or changed, and codes whose
  /// count changed. Empty when the locks are equal.
  List<String> differencesFrom(CorpusLock expected) {
    final keys = {...clients.keys, ...expected.clients.keys}.toList()..sort();
    return [
      for (final key in keys)
        ..._client(key, clients[key], expected.clients[key]),
    ];
  }

  static List<String> _client(String key, ClientLock? got, ClientLock? want) {
    if (want == null) return ['$key: new client'];
    if (got == null) return ['$key: client is gone'];
    return [
      for (final path in _union(got.files, want.files))
        if (got.files[path] != want.files[path])
          '$key: ${_change(got.files[path], want.files[path])} $path',
      for (final code in _union(got.diagnostics, want.diagnostics))
        if (got.diagnostics[code] != want.diagnostics[code])
          '$key: $code ${want.diagnostics[code] ?? 0} -> '
              '${got.diagnostics[code] ?? 0}',
    ];
  }

  static String _change(String? got, String? want) => switch ((got, want)) {
    (null, _) => 'removed',
    (_, null) => 'added',
    _ => 'changed',
  };

  static List<String> _union(Map<String, Object?> a, Map<String, Object?> b) =>
      {...a.keys, ...b.keys}.toList()..sort();
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/corpus_package.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

/// The scratch package of one `union` mode of a corpus run: its pubspec, the
/// analyzer gate's lints and the generator config, with each client's
/// output in `lib/<name>`.
final class CorpusPackage {
  /// Creates the package in [directory] for the `union` mode [mode].
  const CorpusPackage(this.directory, this.mode);

  /// The directory of the package, absolute.
  final String directory;

  /// The `union` mode of the clients: `raw` or `structural`.
  final String mode;

  /// The generator config of the package.
  String get config => p.join(directory, 'typed_openapi.yaml');

  /// The output directory of the client [name].
  String outputOf(String name) => p.join(directory, 'lib', name);

  /// Writes the package for the clients [inputs] (a name and the absolute
  /// path of its spec); [runtime] is the directory of
  /// `typed_openapi_runtime`.
  void write(Map<String, String> inputs, String runtime) {
    Directory(directory).createSync(recursive: true);
    File(
      p.join(directory, 'pubspec.yaml'),
    ).writeAsStringSync(_pubspec(runtime));
    File(
      p.join(directory, 'analysis_options.yaml'),
    ).writeAsStringSync(_analysisOptions);
    final clients = StringBuffer();
    for (final MapEntry(key: name, value: input) in inputs.entries) {
      clients
        ..writeln('  $name:')
        ..writeln('    input: ${_quoted(input)}')
        ..writeln('    output: ${_quoted(outputOf(name))}');
    }
    File(config).writeAsStringSync(
      'defaults:\n  union: $mode\nclients:\n$clients',
    );
  }

  String _pubspec(String runtime) =>
      '''
name: corpus_$mode
publish_to: none
environment:
  sdk: ">=3.10.0 <4.0.0"
dependencies:
  dio: ^5.11.1
  typed_openapi_runtime:
    path: ${_quoted(runtime)}
dev_dependencies:
  lints: ^6.1.0
''';

  /// package:lints/recommended and the rules flutter_lints adds, as in the
  /// analyzer gate of the generator's tests.
  static const _analysisOptions = '''
include: package:lints/recommended.yaml
linter:
  rules:
    - avoid_print
    - avoid_unnecessary_containers
    - avoid_web_libraries_in_flutter
    - no_logic_in_create_state
    - prefer_const_constructors_in_immutables
    - sized_box_for_whitespace
    - sort_child_properties_last
    - use_build_context_synchronously
    - use_full_hex_values_for_flutter_colors
    - use_key_in_widget_constructors
''';

  /// [path] as a single-quoted YAML scalar, with `/` separators.
  static String _quoted(String path) =>
      "'${path.replaceAll(r'\', '/').replaceAll("'", "''")}'";
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/corpus_run.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:typed_openapi/typed_openapi.dart';

import 'client_lock.dart';
import 'corpus_lock.dart';
import 'corpus_package.dart';
import 'corpus_spec.dart';
import 'expectation.dart';
import 'generate_run.dart';
import 'package_gate.dart';
import 'tree_hash.dart';

/// The lock a corpus run computed and the failures of its gates.
typedef CorpusResult = ({CorpusLock lock, List<String> failures});

/// One run of the corpus: for each `union` mode, generates every spec into a
/// fresh package, checks the expectation of each, generates again (which
/// must write nothing), and runs the [PackageGate] on the package.
final class CorpusRun {
  /// Creates the run that works in [work] with the runtime in [runtime],
  /// both absolute.
  const CorpusRun({
    required this.work,
    required this.runtime,
    this.modes = const ['raw', 'structural'],
  });

  /// The directory of the packages, one per mode.
  final String work;

  /// The directory of `typed_openapi_runtime`.
  final String runtime;

  /// The `union` modes to generate.
  final List<String> modes;

  /// The name of the manifest `generate` writes, which is not output code.
  static const String manifest = '.typed_openapi.json';

  /// Runs [specs], whose spec files are [inputs] (by name, absolute).
  Future<CorpusResult> run(
    List<CorpusSpec> specs,
    Map<String, String> inputs,
  ) async {
    final clients = <String, ClientLock>{};
    final failures = <String>[];
    for (final mode in modes) {
      final package = CorpusPackage(p.join(work, mode), mode);
      final lib = Directory(p.join(package.directory, 'lib'));
      if (lib.existsSync()) lib.deleteSync(recursive: true);
      package.write(inputs, runtime);
      final generate = GenerateRun(package.config);
      final first = await generate.generate();
      final checks = {for (final c in generate.check()) c.client.name: c};
      final second = await generate.generate();
      for (final spec in specs) {
        final key = '$mode/${spec.name}';
        final skipped = first.skipped.contains(spec.name);
        failures.addAll(_unexpected(key, spec, skipped, checks[spec.name]));
        final again = second.changed[spec.name] ?? 0;
        if (again > 0) failures.add('$key: a second generate wrote $again.');
        clients[key] = ClientLock(
          files: skipped ? const {} : _files(package.outputOf(spec.name)),
          diagnostics: _counts(checks[spec.name]),
        );
      }
      failures.addAll(await PackageGate(package.directory).run());
    }
    return (lock: CorpusLock(clients), failures: failures);
  }

  static List<String> _unexpected(
    String key,
    CorpusSpec spec,
    bool skipped,
    ClientCheck? check,
  ) {
    final errors = {
      for (final d in check?.diagnostics ?? const <Diagnostic>[])
        if (d.severity == Severity.error) d.code.id,
    }.toList()..sort();
    return switch (spec.expectation) {
      Generates() when skipped => [
        '$key: expected to generate, but it was skipped for $errors.',
      ],
      CheckErrors(:final codes) when !skipped => [
        '$key: expected the errors $codes, but it was generated.',
      ],
      CheckErrors(:final codes) when codes.join() != errors.join() => [
        '$key: expected the errors $codes, but got $errors.',
      ],
      _ => const [],
    };
  }

  static Map<String, String> _files(String output) =>
      const TreeHash().files(output)..remove(manifest);

  static Map<String, int> _counts(ClientCheck? check) {
    final counts = <String, int>{};
    for (final d in check?.diagnostics ?? const <Diagnostic>[]) {
      counts.update(d.code.id, (n) => n + 1, ifAbsent: () => 1);
    }
    return counts;
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/generate_run.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:typed_openapi/typed_openapi.dart';

/// What one `typed_openapi generate` of a corpus package did.
typedef GenerateResult = ({
  Map<String, int> changed,
  Set<String> skipped,
  String report,
});

/// Runs `typed_openapi generate` and `check` in-process on the config of a
/// corpus package.
final class GenerateRun {
  /// Creates the run of the config [config].
  const GenerateRun(this.config);

  /// The generator config.
  final String config;

  static final _generated = RegExp(
    r'^Generated (\S+) in .*: \d+ files?, (\d+) written, (\d+) deleted\.',
  );
  static final _skipped = RegExp(r'^Skipped (\S+):');

  /// Generates every client; [GenerateResult.changed] counts the files each
  /// generated client wrote or deleted, and [GenerateResult.skipped] names
  /// the clients that were not generated.
  Future<GenerateResult> generate() async {
    final out = StringBuffer();
    await TypedOpenApiCli(out: out, err: out).run(['generate', '-c', config]);
    final changed = <String, int>{};
    final skipped = <String>{};
    for (final line in out.toString().split('\n')) {
      if (_generated.firstMatch(line) case final m?) {
        if ((m[1], m[2], m[3]) case (final name?, final w?, final d?)) {
          changed[name] = int.parse(w) + int.parse(d);
        }
      } else if (_skipped.firstMatch(line)?[1] case final name?) {
        skipped.add(name);
      }
    }
    return (changed: changed, skipped: skipped, report: '$out');
  }

  /// The diagnostics of each client of the config, by client name.
  List<ClientCheck> check() {
    final bag = DiagnosticBag();
    final project = ConfigLoader(bag).load(config);
    if (project == null) return const [];
    return SpecChecker(bag).check(project);
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/lock_command.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:args/command_runner.dart';
import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

import 'corpus_exception.dart';
import 'corpus_lock.dart';
import 'corpus_run.dart';
import 'fetch_command.dart';
import 'spec_cache.dart';

/// `corpus update` and `corpus check`: fetch the specs, run the corpus
/// ([CorpusRun]) and write `corpus.lock` (`update`) or compare the run with
/// it (`check`). Both fail when a gate fails; `check` also fails on any
/// difference from the lock and lists the changed files and codes.
final class LockCommand extends Command<int> {
  /// Creates `corpus update`, which rewrites the lock.
  LockCommand.update(this._out, this._err, this._download)
    : name = 'update',
      _writes = true {
    _addOptions();
  }

  /// Creates `corpus check`, which compares the run with the lock.
  LockCommand.check(this._out, this._err, this._download)
    : name = 'check',
      _writes = false {
    _addOptions();
  }

  @override
  final String name;

  final bool _writes;
  final StringSink _out;
  final StringSink _err;
  final Download _download;

  @override
  String get description => _writes
      ? 'Generate the corpus and write corpus.lock.'
      : 'Generate the corpus and compare it with corpus.lock.';

  void _addOptions() {
    FetchCommand.addCorpusOptions(argParser);
    argParser
      ..addOption('lock', defaultsTo: 'corpus.lock', help: 'The lock file.')
      ..addOption(
        'work',
        defaultsTo: '.dart_tool/corpus/out',
        help: 'Where the generated packages are written.',
      )
      ..addOption(
        'runtime',
        defaultsTo: '../typed_openapi_runtime',
        help: 'The typed_openapi_runtime package the output depends on.',
      );
  }

  @override
  Future<int> run() async {
    final results = argResults!;
    final specs = FetchCommand.specsOf(results);
    final cache = SpecCache(results.option('cache')!, _download);
    final CorpusResult result;
    try {
      result =
          await CorpusRun(
            work: p.normalize(p.absolute(results.option('work')!)),
            runtime: p.normalize(p.absolute(results.option('runtime')!)),
          ).run(specs, {
            for (final spec in specs)
              spec.name: p.normalize(p.absolute(await cache.fetch(spec))),
          });
    } on CorpusException catch (e) {
      _err.writeln(e.message);
      return 1;
    }
    final lockFile = File(results.option('lock')!);
    final differences = _writes || !lockFile.existsSync()
        ? <String>[]
        : result.lock.differencesFrom(
            CorpusLock.parse(lockFile.readAsStringSync()),
          );
    if (_writes) lockFile.writeAsStringSync(result.lock.format());
    if (!_writes && !lockFile.existsSync()) {
      differences.add('${lockFile.path} does not exist: run corpus update.');
    }
    result.failures.forEach(_err.writeln);
    differences.forEach(_out.writeln);
    _out.writeln(_summary(result.lock, differences));
    return result.failures.isEmpty && differences.isEmpty ? 0 : 1;
  }

  static String _summary(CorpusLock lock, List<String> differences) {
    final generated = lock.clients.values.where((c) => c.files.isNotEmpty);
    final files = generated.fold(0, (n, c) => n + c.files.length);
    return '${lock.clients.length} clients, ${generated.length} generated, '
        '$files files; ${differences.length} differences from the lock.';
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/package_gate.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:io';

/// The checks of a package of generated code: `dart analyze --fatal-infos`
/// and `dart format --set-exit-if-changed` on its `lib` (generated code
/// carries `// dart format off`, so the formatter of any SDK keeps it).
final class PackageGate {
  /// Creates the gate of the package in [directory].
  const PackageGate(this.directory);

  /// The directory of the package.
  final String directory;

  /// The failures of the checks, one line each; empty when all pass.
  Future<List<String>> run() async {
    final get = await _dart(['pub', 'get', '--offline']);
    if (get.exitCode != 0 && (await _dart(['pub', 'get'])).exitCode != 0) {
      return ['$directory: dart pub get failed: ${get.stderr}'];
    }
    final analyze = await _dart(['analyze', '--fatal-infos']);
    final format = await _dart([
      'format',
      '--output=none',
      '--set-exit-if-changed',
      'lib',
    ]);
    return [
      if (analyze.exitCode != 0)
        '$directory: dart analyze --fatal-infos failed:\n${analyze.stdout}',
      if (format.exitCode != 0)
        '$directory: dart format would change files:\n${format.stdout}',
    ];
  }

  Future<ProcessResult> _dart(List<String> arguments) => Process.run(
    Platform.resolvedExecutable,
    arguments,
    workingDirectory: directory,
  );
}
````

- Produces: `Sample`, `ExampleReader`, `SyntheticValues`, `RoundTripProgram`, `RoundTrip`, `RoundTripResult`; `ClientLock.values`; `CorpusResult.roundTrips`; `CorpusPackage.name` and `clean()`.

Spec decision 3. After the gate, every generated client of each mode gets a round trip: `ExampleReader` takes the `example` and `examples` of each declaration's schema (base variant) and of each JSON response whose type is a declaration (through an envelope's key, and a `#/components/examples` reference), at the locations the IR names. `SyntheticValues` builds from the IR alone: a model with its required fields and one with every field (and a key for `additionalProperties`), an enum's first and last value, a union's every variant with its first discriminator value; constants are taken from `FieldIr.constValue`. `RoundTripProgram` writes `bin/roundtrip_<client>.dart` into the scratch package with a codec per declaration, every import prefixed; each value goes through `fromJson`, `toJson`, JSON text and `fromJson`, and the two results must be `==`. An example that throws `JsonDecodeException` or `FormatException` is a spec defect (counted); any other failure is a failure. The counts go into the lock (`values`), and the failures are printed as `round trip: ...` lines without failing the run (decision 6 of this plan): their number is in the lock.

The first run found 2,061 failures; 2,058 of them were OpenAI values the tool should not have built: OpenAI's unions have a discriminator without a `mapping`, so the IR takes the schema name as the value (`FunctionTool`), while each variant fixes `type` to a `const` (`function`); no JSON selects such a variant (see "Defects found"), and `SyntheticValues` now skips a variant whose model fixes the discriminator property to a constant that none of its values is. Task 5 makes those variants selectable (the user's decision) and drops the skip. The three left are two generator defects, fixed in tasks 7 and 9 (see "Defects found"): GitHub's `ReposGetContentResponse` (`raw` and `structural`) and Medusa's `SearchOptionsFacetsItem` (`structural`).

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/helpers/client_ir.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:typed_openapi/typed_openapi.dart';

import 'temp_dir.dart';

/// The IR of the spec [spec] (YAML), checked as the client `api` of a config
/// in a temp directory, as `generate` would build it.
ClientIr clientIr(String spec) {
  final temp = TempDir();
  temp.write('spec.yaml', spec);
  final config = temp.write(
    'typed_openapi.yaml',
    'clients:\n  api: {input: spec.yaml, output: out}\n',
  );
  final bag = DiagnosticBag();
  final project = ConfigLoader(bag).load(config);
  if (project == null) throw StateError('config: ${bag.all}');
  final ir = SpecChecker(bag).check(project).single.ir;
  if (ir == null) throw StateError('spec: ${bag.all}');
  return ir;
}
````

````dart
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: Pets, version: "1"}
paths:
  /pets:
    get:
      operationId: getPet
      responses:
        "200":
          description: ok
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: {$ref: "#/components/schemas/Pet"}
              example: {id: from-media}
              examples:
                named: {value: {id: named}}
                shared: {$ref: "#/components/examples/Shared"}
components:
  examples:
    Shared: {value: {id: shared}}
  schemas:
    Pet:
      type: object
      properties:
        id: {type: string}
      example: {id: from-schema}
      examples: [{id: listed}]
''';

void main() {
  test('reads the examples of schemas and JSON responses by class', () {
    final samples = ExampleReader().of(clientIr(_spec));
    expect({for (final s in samples) s.type}, {'Pet'});
    expect(samples.every((s) => s.isExample), isTrue);
    expect(
      [for (final s in samples) s.value],
      [
        {'id': 'from-schema'},
        {'id': 'listed'},
        {'id': 'from-media'},
        {'id': 'named'},
        {'id': 'shared'},
      ],
    );
  });
}
````

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/roundtrip/sample.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/roundtrip/synthetic_values.dart';

import '../helpers/client_ir.dart';

const _spec = r'''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: Pets, version: "1"}
paths:
  /pets:
    get:
      operationId: getPet
      responses:
        "200":
          description: ok
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: {$ref: "#/components/schemas/Pet"}
components:
  schemas:
    Pet:
      oneOf:
        - {$ref: "#/components/schemas/Cat"}
        - {$ref: "#/components/schemas/Dog"}
      discriminator:
        propertyName: kind
        mapping: {cat: "#/components/schemas/Cat", dog: "#/components/schemas/Dog"}
    Cat:
      type: object
      required: [kind, born]
      properties:
        kind: {type: string}
        born: {type: string, format: date}
        tags: {type: array, items: {type: string}}
    Dog:
      type: object
      required: [kind]
      properties:
        kind: {type: string}
        size: {$ref: "#/components/schemas/Size"}
    Size: {type: string, enum: [small, medium, large]}
''';

void main() {
  late List<Sample> samples;
  setUp(() => samples = SyntheticValues(clientIr(_spec)).all());

  /// The values of the class [type].
  List<Object?> valuesOf(String type) => [
    for (final s in samples)
      if (s.type == type) s.value,
  ];

  test('a model has a value of its required fields and one of all', () {
    expect(valuesOf('Cat'), [
      {'kind': 'text', 'born': '2026-01-02'},
      {
        'kind': 'text',
        'born': '2026-01-02',
        'tags': ['text'],
      },
    ]);
  });

  test('an enum has its first and its last value', () {
    expect(valuesOf('Size'), ['small', 'large']);
  });

  test('each variant of a union carries its discriminator value', () {
    expect(valuesOf('Pet'), [
      {'kind': 'cat', 'born': '2026-01-02'},
      {'kind': 'dog'},
    ]);
  });

  test('no synthetic value is an example', () {
    expect(samples.where((s) => s.isExample), isEmpty);
  });
}
````
