**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/roundtrip/example_reader.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:typed_openapi/typed_openapi.dart';

import 'sample.dart';

/// The examples of a spec, each as a [Sample] of the class the IR gives its
/// schema: `example` and `examples` of every declaration's schema, and of
/// the JSON media type of every response whose body is a declaration.
///
/// The IR says which class a value belongs to; the reader only looks up the
/// example keys at the locations the IR names.
final class ExampleReader {
  /// Creates the reader; documents are loaded once per file.
  ExampleReader();

  final _documents = <Uri, SpecDocument?>{};

  /// The examples of the client [ir].
  List<Sample> of(ClientIr ir) {
    final names = {for (final d in ir.declarations) d.id: d.name};
    return [
      for (final d in ir.declarations)
        if (d.id.variant == DeclVariant.base)
          for (final value in _schemaExamples(d.id.location))
            Sample(d.name, value, isExample: true),
      for (final group in ir.groups)
        for (final operation in group.operations)
          for (final response in operation.responses)
            if (response.type case NamedType(:final id))
              if (names[id] case final name?)
                for (final value in _mediaExamples(response))
                  Sample(name, value, isExample: true),
    ];
  }

  List<Object?> _schemaExamples(SpecLocation location) {
    final schema = _node(location.document, location.pointer);
    if (schema is! Map<String, Object?>) return const [];
    return [
      ?schema['example'],
      if (schema['examples'] case final List<Object?> examples)
        for (final example in examples) ?example,
    ];
  }

  List<Object?> _mediaExamples(ResponseIr response) {
    final type = response.contentType;
    if (type == null || !type.contains('json')) return const [];
    final location = response.location;
    final media = _node(
      location.document,
      location.pointer.child('content').child(type),
    );
    if (media is! Map<String, Object?>) return const [];
    final values = [
      ?media['example'],
      if (media['examples'] case final Map<String, Object?> examples)
        for (final example in examples.values)
          if (_resolve(location.document, example) case {'value': final v?}) v,
    ];
    final key = response.envelopeKey;
    return [
      for (final value in values)
        if (key == null)
          value
        else if (value case final Map<String, Object?> envelope)
          ?envelope[key],
    ];
  }

  Object? _resolve(Uri document, Object? node) => switch (node) {
    {r'$ref': final String ref} when ref.startsWith('#/') => _node(
      document,
      JsonPointer.parse(ref),
    ),
    _ => node,
  };

  Object? _node(Uri document, JsonPointer pointer) {
    final loaded = _documents.putIfAbsent(
      document,
      () => SpecLoader(DiagnosticBag()).load(document.toFilePath()),
    );
    return loaded?.valueAt(pointer)?.$1;
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/roundtrip/round_trip.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:typed_openapi/typed_openapi.dart';

import 'example_reader.dart';
import 'roundtrip_program.dart';
import 'synthetic_values.dart';

/// What the round trip of one client found: the examples and synthetic
/// values that decoded, the examples that do not (defects of the spec), and
/// the failures (of the generator or of the tool).
typedef RoundTripResult = ({
  int examples,
  int synthetic,
  List<String> defects,
  List<String> failures,
});

/// Runs the runtime decoding test of one generated client (R15.3) in the
/// package that holds its code, after `dart pub get`.
final class RoundTrip {
  /// Creates the round trip in the package [directory] named [package].
  const RoundTrip(this.directory, this.package);

  /// The directory of the package of generated code.
  final String directory;

  /// The name of the package, for its `package:` imports.
  final String package;

  /// Round-trips the examples [examples] reads and the synthetic values of
  /// the client [client] with the IR [ir].
  Future<RoundTripResult> run(
    String client,
    ClientIr ir,
    ExampleReader examples,
  ) async {
    final samples = [...examples.of(ir), ...SyntheticValues(ir).all()];
    final data = p.join(directory, 'roundtrip', '$client.json');
    File(data)
      ..createSync(recursive: true)
      ..writeAsStringSync(jsonEncode([for (final s in samples) s.toJson()]));
    final program = p.join('bin', 'roundtrip_$client.dart');
    File(p.join(directory, program))
      ..createSync(recursive: true)
      ..writeAsStringSync(
        RoundTripProgram(ir).source('package:$package/$client/models.dart'),
      );
    final result = await Process.run(Platform.resolvedExecutable, [
      'run',
      program,
      data,
    ], workingDirectory: directory);
    return _read(client, result);
  }

  /// The result the program printed as its last line.
  static RoundTripResult _read(String client, ProcessResult result) {
    final lines = '${result.stdout}'.trim().split('\n');
    if (result.exitCode != 0 || lines.last.isEmpty) {
      return (
        examples: 0,
        synthetic: 0,
        defects: const <String>[],
        failures: ['$client: the round trip did not run: ${result.stderr}'],
      );
    }
    final json = jsonDecode(lines.last) as Map<String, Object?>;
    List<String> texts(String key) => [
      for (final text in json[key] as List<Object?>) '$client: $text',
    ];
    return (
      examples: json['examples'] as int,
      synthetic: json['synthetic'] as int,
      defects: texts('defects'),
      failures: texts('failures'),
    );
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/roundtrip/roundtrip_program.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:typed_openapi/typed_openapi.dart';

/// The Dart program that round-trips the samples of one generated client:
/// each value goes through `fromJson`, `toJson`, JSON text and `fromJson`
/// again, and the second result must equal the first (spec decision 3).
///
/// The program reads the samples from the file its argument names and
/// prints one JSON object: `examples` and `synthetic` (the values that
/// decoded), `defects` (examples that do not decode: defects of the spec)
/// and `failures` (everything else that went wrong). Every import is
/// prefixed, so no generated name can clash with the program's.
final class RoundTripProgram {
  /// Creates the program for the declarations of [ir].
  const RoundTripProgram(this._ir);

  final ClientIr _ir;

  /// The source of the program, which imports the models of the client from
  /// [library] (`package:corpus_raw/stripe/models.dart`).
  String source(String library) {
    final codecs = StringBuffer();
    for (final d in _ir.declarations) {
      final decode = switch (d) {
        ModelDecl() => '(j) => m.${d.name}.fromJson(rt.JsonReader.asObject(j))',
        EnumDecl() || UnionDecl() => 'm.${d.name}.fromJson',
      };
      codecs.writeln(
        "  '${d.name}': (decode: $decode, "
        'encode: (v) => (v as m.${d.name}).toJson()),',
      );
    }
    return '''
// The round trip of a corpus client, written by the corpus tool.
import 'dart:convert' as convert;
import 'dart:io' as io;

import '$library' as m;
import 'package:typed_openapi_runtime/typed_openapi_runtime.dart' as rt;

typedef RoundTripCodec = ({
  Object? Function(Object?) decode,
  Object? Function(Object?) encode,
});

final codecs = <String, RoundTripCodec>{
$codecs};

$_main''';
  }

  static const _main = r'''
void main(List<String> args) {
  final samples = convert.jsonDecode(io.File(args.single).readAsStringSync());
  var examples = 0;
  var synthetic = 0;
  final defects = <String>[];
  final failures = <String>[];
  for (final sample in (samples as List<Object?>).cast<Map<String, Object?>>()) {
    final type = sample['type'] as String;
    final isExample = sample['example'] == true;
    final value = sample['value'];
    final codec = codecs[type]!;
    final Object? first;
    try {
      first = codec.decode(value);
    } on rt.JsonDecodeException catch (e) {
      (isExample ? defects : failures).add('$type: $e');
      continue;
    } on FormatException catch (e) {
      (isExample ? defects : failures).add('$type: $e');
      continue;
    } catch (e) {
      failures.add('$type: decoding ${convert.jsonEncode(value)} threw $e');
      continue;
    }
    isExample ? examples++ : synthetic++;
    try {
      final text = convert.jsonEncode(codec.encode(first));
      final second = codec.decode(convert.jsonDecode(text));
      if (second != first) {
        failures.add('$type: ${convert.jsonEncode(value)} came back as $text');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      failures.add('$type: the round trip of ${convert.jsonEncode(value)} threw $e');
    }
  }
  io.stdout.writeln(convert.jsonEncode({
    'examples': examples,
    'synthetic': synthetic,
    'defects': defects,
    'failures': failures,
  }));
}
''';
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/roundtrip/sample.dart`:

````dart
/// One JSON value the round trip decodes as the generated class [type]:
/// an example of the spec, or a synthetic value built from the IR.
final class Sample {
  /// Creates the sample of [value] for [type].
  const Sample(this.type, this.value, {required this.isExample});

  /// The name of the generated class (a declaration of the IR).
  final String type;

  /// The JSON value.
  final Object? value;

  /// Whether the value is an example of the spec; a spec example that does
  /// not decode is a defect of the spec, a synthetic value one of the tool
  /// or the generator.
  final bool isExample;

  /// The sample as JSON, as the round-trip program reads it.
  Map<String, Object?> toJson() => {
    'type': type,
    'example': isExample,
    'value': value,
  };
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/roundtrip/synthetic_values.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:typed_openapi/typed_openapi.dart';

import 'sample.dart';

/// JSON values built from the IR of a client (spec decision 3): for each
/// model one with its required fields and one with every field, for each
/// enum its first and last value, for each union one value of each variant
/// (with its discriminator value).
///
/// A value is a record `(json,)`, so that JSON `null` is a value; null means
/// that no value can be built (a custom type, or nesting deeper than
/// [maxDepth] in a required field).
final class SyntheticValues {
  /// Creates the values of the client [ir].
  SyntheticValues(this._ir)
    : _byId = {for (final d in _ir.declarations) d.id: d};

  /// How deep declarations nest in one value.
  static const int maxDepth = 6;

  final ClientIr _ir;
  final Map<DeclId, Declaration> _byId;

  /// The values of every declaration.
  List<Sample> all() => [
    for (final d in _ir.declarations)
      for (final (value,) in _valuesOf(d))
        Sample(d.name, value, isExample: false),
  ];

  List<(Object?,)> _valuesOf(Declaration d) => switch (d) {
    ModelDecl() => [?_model(d, 0, every: false), ?_model(d, 0, every: true)],
    EnumDecl(:final values) when values.isEmpty => const [],
    EnumDecl(:final values) => [
      (values.first.value,),
      if (values.length > 1) (values.last.value,),
    ],
    UnionDecl(:final variants) => [
      for (final variant in variants) ?_variant(d, variant, 0),
    ],
  };

  (Object?,)? _model(ModelDecl model, int depth, {required bool every}) {
    final json = <String, Object?>{};
    for (final field in model.fields) {
      if (!field.required && !every) continue;
      final value = switch (field.constValue) {
        final constant? => (constant,),
        null => _value(field.valueType, depth + 1),
      };
      if (value == null) {
        if (field.required) return null;
        continue;
      }
      json[field.jsonKey] = value.$1;
    }
    final additional = every ? model.additionalProperties : null;
    if (additional != null) {
      if (_value(additional, depth + 1) case (final extra,)) {
        json['__extra'] = extra;
      }
    }
    return (json,);
  }

  (Object?,)? _variant(UnionDecl union, UnionVariantIr variant, int depth) {
    if (variant.jsonKinds.isNotEmpty) {
      return (
        switch (variant.jsonKinds.first) {
          .string => 'text',
          .integer => 1,
          .number => 2.5,
          .boolean => true,
          .array => <Object?>[],
          .object => <String, Object?>{},
        },
      );
    }
    final value = _value(variant.type, depth + 1);
    final property = union.discriminator;
    if (property == null || value == null) return value;
    final values = variant.discriminatorValues;
    if (values.isEmpty || _fixes(variant.type, property, values)) return null;
    return switch (value.$1) {
      final Map<String, Object?> object => (
        {...object, property: variant.discriminatorValues.first},
      ),
      _ => value,
    };
  }

  /// Whether the model [type] fixes the discriminator [property] to a
  /// constant that none of the variant's discriminator [values] is: no JSON
  /// selects such a variant (OpenAI's unions without a mapping).
  bool _fixes(DartType type, String property, List<String> values) =>
      switch (type) {
        NamedType(:final id) => switch (_byId[id]) {
          ModelDecl(:final fields) => fields.any(
            (f) =>
                f.jsonKey == property &&
                f.constValue != null &&
                !values.contains(f.constValue),
          ),
          _ => false,
        },
        _ => false,
      };

  (Object?,)? _value(DartType type, int depth) {
    if (depth > maxDepth) return null;
    return switch (type) {
      CoreType(:final kind) => _core(kind),
      ListType(:final item) => ([?_value(item, depth + 1)?.$1],),
      MapType(:final value) => switch (_value(value, depth + 1)) {
        (final v,) => ({'key': v},),
        null => (<String, Object?>{},),
      },
      NamedType(:final id) => switch (_byId[id]) {
        final ModelDecl model => _model(model, depth, every: false),
        EnumDecl(:final values) when values.isNotEmpty => (values.first.value,),
        final UnionDecl union => _firstVariant(union, depth),
        _ => null,
      },
      PatchType(:final value) => _value(value, depth),
      CustomType() => null,
    };
  }

  (Object?,)? _firstVariant(UnionDecl union, int depth) {
    for (final variant in union.variants) {
      if (_variant(union, variant, depth) case final value?) return value;
    }
    return null;
  }

  static (Object?,)? _core(CoreKind kind) => switch (kind) {
    .integer => (1,),
    .float => (1.5,),
    .number => (2.5,),
    .string => ('text',),
    .boolean => (true,),
    .dateTime => ('2026-01-02T03:04:05.000Z',),
    .date => ('2026-01-02',),
    .bytes => ('AQID',),
    .binary => null,
    .json => (
      {
        'any': [1, 'two'],
      },
    ),
  };
}
````

````diff
+  Future<CorpusResult> _mode(
+    String mode,
+    List<CorpusSpec> specs,

+    final package = CorpusPackage(p.join(work, mode), mode);

+      failures: failures,
+      roundTrips: roundTrips,

+    return {

+        if (check.ir case final ir? when !skipped.contains(name))
+          name: await trip.run(name, ir, examples),
+    };
+  }
+
+  static Map<String, int> _values(RoundTripResult trip) => {
+    'examples': trip.examples,
+    'failures': trip.failures.length,

+    'synthetic': trip.synthetic,
+  };
````

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the lock**

Run from `packages/typed_openapi_tool`: `dart run bin/corpus.dart update`.
Expected: exit code 0, the summary `40 clients, 38 generated, 27759 files; 0 differences from the lock.`, and exactly these three lines on stderr:

```text
round trip: raw/github: ReposGetContentResponse: JsonDecodeException: expected an object, got an array in ReposGetContentResponse
round trip: structural/github: ReposGetContentResponse: JsonDecodeException: expected an object, got an array in ReposGetContentResponse
round trip: structural/medusa: SearchOptionsFacetsItem: {"field":"text"} came back as {"field":"text","type":"value"}
```

The lock now has `values` for every generated client; over the corpus `raw` checks 1,762 examples (136 spec defects) and 25,551 synthetic values, `structural` 1,780 examples (136 spec defects) and 28,865 synthetic values (see "Corpus run").

### Task 5: A discriminator variant is selected by the value it fixes (TOA457, open question 4: user decision)

**Files:**
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/CHANGELOG.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diagnostics/diagnostic_code.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/discriminator_reader.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/union_notices.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/discriminator_derived_test.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/roundtrip/synthetic_values.dart`
- Write (by `dart run bin/corpus.dart update`): `packages/typed_openapi_tool/corpus.lock`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: M8 `DiscriminatorReader` (`_fromMapping`, `_fromVariants`, `_constantProperty`, which already reads a `const` or a one-value `enum` through `$ref`s and `allOf` parts), `UnionNotices`, `DeclarationNames.componentKey`; task 4 (`SyntheticValues`).
- Produces: `DiagnosticCode.discriminatorValueDerived` (TOA457, `discriminator-value-derived`, info); `UnionNotices.valueDerived(String value, String key, Schema variant)`; `UnionVariantIr.discriminatorValues` of a `$ref` variant is the value its discriminator property is fixed to, when the `mapping` does not name it.

The user decided open question 4 of the first draft: when the `mapping` has no entry for a variant and the variant fixes the discriminator property to one value (a single `const` or a one-value `enum`, also through a `$ref` or an `allOf` part), that value selects it, not the component name OpenAPI implies. Inline variants already worked so (M8); this extends it to `$ref` variants. An explicit `mapping` entry always wins (`_fromMapping` runs first and the variant is skipped); two variants that derive the same value are the existing TOA456 (the earlier decodes it). TOA457 (info) is reported for each derived value that differs from the component name, at the variant; it takes the next free code of the unions' second half (TOA450-TOA456). OpenAI's unions now decode real payloads (`{"type": "function"}` is a `FunctionTool`): 753 TOA457 in the `raw` corpus (and 2 at artemis, whose client is skipped), 4 more TOA456 where two OpenAI variants fix one value. `SyntheticValues` no longer skips variants whose constant no value matched: 1,654 more synthetic values are checked (807 `raw`, 847 `structural`), all of them come back equal. No golden changes; the corpus `openai` client changes in 110 (`raw`) and 114 (`structural`) files, so the lock is written again and the CHANGELOG gets "Output changes".

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/discriminator_derived_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diagnostics/diagnostic_code.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diagnostics/severity.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/ir/ir.dart';

import '../helpers/ir_fixture.dart';
import '../helpers/mapping.dart';

/// The discriminator values of each variant of the union [name] of [ir].
List<List<String>> valuesOf(ClientIr ir, String name) => [
  for (final v
      in (ir.declarations.firstWhere((d) => d.name == name) as UnionDecl)
          .variants)
    v.discriminatorValues,
];

/// A spec whose union `Tool` has the variants [variants] and the
/// discriminator [discriminator], next to the components [components].
String spec(String variants, String discriminator, String components) =>
    '''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
components:
  schemas:
    Tool:
      oneOf:
$variants
      discriminator: $discriminator
$components''';

const _components = r'''
    FunctionTool:
      type: object
      required: [type]
      properties:
        type: {const: function}
        name: {type: string}
    FileSearch:
      allOf:
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/Base'
        - type: object
          properties:
            type: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/FileSearchType'}
    FileSearchType: {type: string, enum: [file_search]}
    Base: {type: object, properties: {id: {type: string}}}
    Plain: {type: object, properties: {type: {type: string}}}
''';

void main() {
  test('a variant that fixes the property is selected by that value', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(
      spec(
        r'''
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/FunctionTool'
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/FileSearch'
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/Plain'
''',
        '{propertyName: type}',
        _components,
      ),
    );
    final (ir, _) = mapComponents(fixture, ['Tool']);
    expect(valuesOf(ir, 'Tool'), [
      ['function'],
      ['file_search'],
      ['Plain'],
    ]);
    final derived = fixture.reportedOf(
      DiagnosticCode.discriminatorValueDerived,
    );
    expect(
      [for (final d in derived) d.pointer?.value],
      [
        '#/components/schemas/Tool/oneOf/0',
        '#/components/schemas/Tool/oneOf/1',
      ],
    );
    expect(derived.first.severity, Severity.info);
    expect(derived.first.message, contains('"function"'));
    expect(derived.first.message, contains('"FunctionTool"'));
  });

  test('an explicit mapping wins over the constant', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(
      spec(
        r'''
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/FunctionTool'
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/FileSearch'
''',
        "{propertyName: type, mapping: {fn: '#/components/schemas/FunctionTool'}}",
        _components,
      ),
    );
    final (ir, _) = mapComponents(fixture, ['Tool']);
    expect(valuesOf(ir, 'Tool'), [
      ['fn'],
      ['file_search'],
    ]);
    expect(
      fixture.reportedOf(DiagnosticCode.discriminatorValueDerived),
      hasLength(1),
    );
  });

  test('two variants that derive one value are TOA456', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(
      spec(
        r'''
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/FunctionTool'
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/OtherFunction'
''',
        '{propertyName: type}',
        '$_components    OtherFunction: '
            '{type: object, properties: {type: {const: function}}}\n',
      ),
    );
    final (ir, _) = mapComponents(fixture, ['Tool']);
    expect(valuesOf(ir, 'Tool'), [
      ['function'],
      ['function'],
    ]);
    expect(
      fixture.reportedOf(DiagnosticCode.discriminatorValueDuplicate),
      hasLength(1),
    );
  });

  test('a constant equal to the component name is not reported', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(
      spec(
        r'''
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/Same'
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/Plain'
''',
        '{propertyName: type}',
        '$_components    Same: '
            '{type: object, properties: {type: {const: Same}}}\n',
      ),
    );
    final (ir, _) = mapComponents(fixture, ['Tool']);
    expect(valuesOf(ir, 'Tool'), [
      ['Same'],
      ['Plain'],
    ]);
    expect(
      fixture.reportedOf(DiagnosticCode.discriminatorValueDerived),
      isEmpty,
    );
  });
}
````

### Task 6: Room in the union emitter: the discriminator decoder moves out (refactor)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/discriminator_decoder.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/union_emitter.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/union_variants.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/quality/size_limits_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: M8 `UnionEmitter` (`_byDiscriminator`, `_decode`, `_wrap`, `_remembers`), `TypeCodec`.
- Produces: `final class UnionVariants { const UnionVariants(TypeCodec); String decode(DartType, String json); String wrap(UnionVariantIr, String value); bool remembers(UnionDecl, UnionVariantIr); }`; `final class DiscriminatorDecoder { const DiscriminatorDecoder(TypeCodec, UnionVariants); void write(DartWriter, UnionDecl); }` (with `_cases` and `_otherwise`, each under 40 lines).

`union_emitter.dart` is allowlisted (416 lines) and `UnionEmitter._byDiscriminator` too (61 lines), so task 7 cannot change them. The `fromJson` of a discriminator union moves into `DiscriminatorDecoder`, split into the cases and the last case; what the union writers share moves into `UnionVariants`. No byte of output changes: the goldens pass, and `corpus check` finds no difference from task 5's lock. The ratchet goes down: `union_emitter.dart` 416 -> 333, the `_byDiscriminator` entry is removed.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the refactor**

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/discriminator_decoder.dart`:

````dart
import '../ir/ir.dart';
import 'dart_code.dart';
import 'dart_writer.dart';
import 'type_codec.dart';
import 'union_variants.dart';

/// Writes the `fromJson` of a union that dispatches by a discriminator
/// property (R9.1): a `switch` on the property's value, with the default
/// mapping's variant or the unknown variant for any other value.
final class DiscriminatorDecoder {
  /// Creates the writer that writes with [_codec] and [_variants].
  const DiscriminatorDecoder(this._codec, this._variants);

  final TypeCodec _codec;
  final UnionVariants _variants;

  static const _code = DartCode();

  /// Writes the `fromJson` factory of [decl] to [out].
  void write(DartWriter out, UnionDecl decl) {
    final name = decl.name;
    final property = decl.discriminator!;
    _codec.imports.fromRuntime('JsonReader');
    out
      ..prose(
        'Decodes a [$name] by its `${_code.docSpan(property)}`; a value no '
        'variant has is a [${decl.unknownName}].',
      )
      ..block('factory $name.fromJson(Object? json)', () {
        out
          ..line(
            'final r = JsonReader.of(json, schema: ${_code.string(name)});',
          )
          ..line('return switch (r.json[${_code.string(property)}]) {')
          ..indented(() {
            _cases(decl).forEach(out.line);
            out.line('${_otherwise(decl)},');
          })
          ..line('};');
      });
  }

  /// A case per variant that a discriminator value selects first.
  List<String> _cases(UnionDecl decl) {
    final seen = <String>{};
    final cases = <String>[];
    for (final variant in decl.variants) {
      final values = [
        for (final value in variant.discriminatorValues)
          if (seen.add(value)) _code.string(value),
      ];
      if (values.isEmpty) continue;
      final decoded = _variants.decode(variant.type, 'r.json');
      if (_variants.remembers(decl, variant)) {
        final pattern = values.length == 1
            ? values.single
            : '(${values.join(' || ')})';
        cases.add(
          '$pattern && final String tag => '
          '${variant.name}._($decoded, tag),',
        );
      } else {
        cases.add(
          '${values.join(' || ')} => ${_variants.wrap(variant, decoded)},',
        );
      }
    }
    return cases;
  }

  /// The last case: the default mapping's variant, or the unknown one.
  String _otherwise(UnionDecl decl) {
    final fallback = switch (decl.defaultVariant) {
      final type? => decl.variants.firstWhere((v) => v.type == type),
      null => null,
    };
    if (fallback == null) {
      return '_ => ${decl.unknownName}._(JsonValue(r.json))';
    }
    final decoded = _variants.decode(fallback.type, 'r.json');
    if (_variants.remembers(decl, fallback)) {
      return 'final tag => ${fallback.name}._($decoded, '
          'tag is String ? tag : null)';
    }
    return '_ => ${_variants.wrap(fallback, decoded)}';
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/union_variants.dart`:

````dart
import '../ir/dart_type.dart';
import '../ir/ir.dart';
import 'type_codec.dart';

/// What the writers of a union share about its variants: how a variant's
/// value is decoded and wrapped, and whether its wrapper keeps the
/// discriminator value it was decoded with.
final class UnionVariants {
  /// Creates the helper that writes with [_codec].
  const UnionVariants(this._codec);

  final TypeCodec _codec;

  /// The value of [type] decoded from [json], a JSON expression.
  String decode(DartType type, String json) {
    final code = _codec.decodeValue(type, json);
    if (code.contains('JsonReader.')) _codec.imports.fromRuntime('JsonReader');
    return code;
  }

  /// [value] wrapped in the class of [variant].
  String wrap(UnionVariantIr variant, String value) =>
      '${variant.name}($value)';

  /// Whether the wrapper of [variant] writes the discriminator itself, as
  /// its model has no property for it (decision 5), and so keeps the value
  /// it was decoded with: an alias (`kitty` for `Cat`) is written back as
  /// it came, not changed into the first value of the mapping. A variant
  /// that is itself a union (`Pet` -> `Dog` -> `Lab`) has no property to
  /// carry the value either, unless it dispatches by the same property and
  /// writes it itself.
  bool remembers(UnionDecl decl, UnionVariantIr variant) {
    final property = decl.discriminator;
    final type = variant.type;
    if (property == null || type is! NamedType) return false;
    return switch (_codec.declaration(type.id)) {
      final ModelDecl model => !model.fields.any((f) => f.jsonKey == property),
      final UnionDecl union => union.discriminator != property,
      _ => false,
    };
  }
}
````

### Task 7: Fix from the round trip: a non-object variant of a discriminator union is picked by its JSON type (GitHub `repos/get-content`)

**Files:**
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/CHANGELOG.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/discriminator_decoder.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/discriminator_array_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/fixtures/discriminator_array.yaml`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/generated_code_test.dart`
- Write (by `dart run bin/corpus.dart update`): `packages/typed_openapi_tool/corpus.lock`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: task 6 (`DiscriminatorDecoder`, `UnionVariants`); M8 `TypeCodec.jsonPattern`.
- Produces: `DiscriminatorDecoder` writes, before it reads the property, `if (json case final <pattern> value) { return <Variant>(<decode>(value)); }` for the first variant of each JSON type that is not an object, and leaves those variants out of the `switch`.

GitHub's `repos/get-content` is a `oneOf` of a directory (a JSON array of items), a file, a symbolic link and a submodule with `discriminator: {propertyName: type, mapping: {array: ..., file: ...}}`. The decoder read the property from an object first, so a real directory listing was a `JsonDecodeException` (the round trip's synthetic array value, and one of GitHub's own examples, which the lock had counted as a spec defect). A variant whose JSON pattern (`TypeCodec.jsonPattern`) is not `Map<String, Object?>` (an array, a string, a number, a boolean, an enum) has no property to read, so it is picked by its JSON type first; object variants, `JsonValue` and nested unions keep the `switch`. The fixture `discriminator_array.yaml` (GitHub's shape) joins the analyzer gate. Only that shape changes: in the corpus `repos_get_content_response.dart` of `github` (both modes); GitHub's example now decodes (`examples` 685 -> 686, `spec_defects` 125 -> 124, `failures` 1 -> 0 per mode).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/discriminator_array_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/config/generation_config.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/overrides/override_set.dart';

import '../helpers/emit_fixture.dart';

/// The `fromJson` factory of the class [name] in the files [files].
String fromJsonOf(Map<String, String> files, String name) {
  final source = files.values.join('\n');
  final start = source.indexOf('factory $name.fromJson');
  if (start < 0) throw StateError('no $name.fromJson');
  return source.substring(start, source.indexOf('\n  }\n', start));
}

void main() {
  for (final mode in UnionMode.values) {
    test('a non-object variant is picked by JSON type first ($mode)', () {
      final factory = fromJsonOf(
        emitFixture(
          'discriminator_array.yaml',
          generation: GenerationConfig(union: mode),
        ),
        'Content',
      );
      final byType = factory.indexOf(
        'if (json case final List<Object?> value)',
      );
      expect(byType, greaterThan(0));
      expect(
        factory,
        contains(
          'return ContentList(JsonReader.listOf('
          'JsonReader.objectOf(Item.fromJson))(value));',
        ),
      );
      expect(byType, lessThan(factory.indexOf('JsonReader.of(json')));
      expect(factory, isNot(contains("'array' =>")));
      expect(factory, contains("'file' => ContentFile("));
    });
  }
}
````

````yaml
openapi: 3.0.3
````

### Task 8: Room in the model emitter: constructors and `fromJson` move out (refactor)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/model_constructors.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/model_emitter.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/quality/size_limits_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: M8 `ModelEmitter` (`write`, `_constructor`, `_parameter`, `_fromJson`, `_known`, `_item`, `_read`, `_isJson`).
- Produces: `final class ModelConstructors { const ModelConstructors(TypeCodec); void write(DartWriter, ModelDecl, List<FieldIr> fields, List<FieldIr> constants); static bool isJson(DartType); }` (`_fromJson` split into `_check` and `_arguments`).

`model_emitter.dart` is allowlisted (379 lines), and so are `ModelEmitter._fromJson` (46) and `ModelEmitter.write` (57), so task 9 cannot change them. The constructors and `fromJson` move into `ModelConstructors`; `model_emitter.dart` drops to 255 lines and leaves the allowlist, `_fromJson` leaves it, and `write` goes down to 46. No byte of output changes: the goldens pass, and `corpus check` finds no difference from task 7's lock.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the refactor**

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/model_constructors.dart`:

````dart
import '../ir/dart_type.dart';
import '../ir/ir.dart';
import 'dart_code.dart';
import 'dart_writer.dart';
import 'type_codec.dart';

/// Writes how a model is made (decisions 3, 8 and 13 of M8): its `const`
/// constructors and its `fromJson` factory.
///
/// A field of a model sent in requests that holds an enum takes only the
/// known values in the constructor (R8.2); `fromJson`, which may meet
/// unknown ones, then uses a private constructor `_`.
final class ModelConstructors {
  /// Creates the writer that writes with [_codec].
  const ModelConstructors(this._codec);

  final TypeCodec _codec;

  static const _code = DartCode();

  /// Writes the constructors and `fromJson` of [model], whose fields are
  /// [fields] and whose constant fields are [constants], to [out].
  void write(
    DartWriter out,
    ModelDecl model,
    List<FieldIr> fields,
    List<FieldIr> constants,
  ) {
    final name = model.name;
    final extra = model.additionalProperties;
    final known =
        model.direction != .response &&
        (fields.any((f) => _codec.hasEnum(f.type)) ||
            (extra != null && _codec.hasEnum(extra)));
    out.prose('Creates a [$name].');
    _constructor(out, 'const $name', model, fields, typed: known);
    if (known) {
      out.line();
      _constructor(out, 'const $name._', model, fields, typed: false);
    }
    out.line();
    _fromJson(out, model, (fields, constants), private: known);
  }

  /// Whether [type] is `JsonValue`, which is never null.
  static bool isJson(DartType type) =>
      type is CoreType && type.kind == CoreKind.json;

  void _constructor(
    DartWriter out,
    String head,
    ModelDecl model,
    List<FieldIr> fields, {
    required bool typed,
  }) {
    final params = [
      for (final field in fields) _parameter(field, typed: typed),
      if (model.additionalProperties case final extra?)
        '${typed && _codec.hasEnum(extra) ? '${_codec.known(MapType(extra))} ' : ''}'
            'this.${model.additionalPropertiesName} = const {}',
    ];
    if (params.isEmpty) {
      out.line('$head();');
    } else {
      out.list('$head({', params, '});');
    }
  }

  String _parameter(FieldIr field, {required bool typed}) {
    final type = field.type;
    final declared = typed && _codec.hasEnum(type)
        ? '${_codec.known(type)} '
        : '';
    if (field.required) return 'required ${declared}this.${field.name}';
    if (isJson(type)) {
      _codec.imports.fromRuntime('JsonValue');
      return '${declared}this.${field.name} = const JsonValue(null)';
    }
    return '${declared}this.${field.name}';
  }

  /// Writes `fromJson`; [split] is the fields and the constant fields.
  void _fromJson(
    DartWriter out,
    ModelDecl model,
    (List<FieldIr>, List<FieldIr>) split, {
    required bool private,
  }) {
    final (fields, constants) = split;
    final name = model.name;
    final create = private ? '$name._' : name;
    out.prose(
      'Decodes a [$name] from [json]; throws a `JsonDecodeException` '
      'when it does not match the schema.',
    );
    final head = 'factory $name.fromJson(Map<String, Object?> json)';
    if (fields.isEmpty &&
        constants.isEmpty &&
        model.additionalProperties == null) {
      out.line('$head => const $create();');
      return;
    }
    _codec.imports.fromRuntime('JsonReader');
    out.block(head, () {
      out.line('final r = JsonReader(json, schema: ${_code.string(name)});');
      constants.map(_check).forEach(out.line);
      final arguments = _arguments(model, fields);
      if (arguments.isEmpty) {
        out.line('return const $create();');
      } else {
        out.list('return $create(', arguments, ');');
      }
    });
  }

  /// The statement of `fromJson` that checks the constant [field].
  String _check(FieldIr field) {
    final value = _code.literal(field.constValue);
    return field.required
        ? 'r.constant(${_code.string(field.jsonKey)}, $value);'
        : 'r.constant(${_code.string(field.jsonKey)}, $value, '
              'required: false);';
  }

  /// The named arguments of the constructor `fromJson` calls.
  List<String> _arguments(ModelDecl model, List<FieldIr> fields) => [
    for (final field in fields) '${field.name}: ${_read(field)}',
    if (model.additionalProperties case final extra?)
      '${model.additionalPropertiesName}: r.additional(${_known(model)}, '
          '${_item(extra)})',
  ];

  /// The set of the JSON keys of [model]'s properties, as a constant.
  String _known(ModelDecl model) {
    final keys = [for (final f in model.fields) _code.string(f.jsonKey)];
    return 'const {${keys.join(', ')}}';
  }

  String _item(DartType type) => type.nullable && !isJson(type)
      ? 'JsonReader.nullable(${_codec.decoder(type)})'
      : _codec.decoder(type);

  String _read(FieldIr field) {
    final key = _code.string(field.jsonKey);
    final value = field.valueType;
    if (field.triState) {
      if (isJson(value)) return 'r.patchValue($key)';
      return 'r.patch($key, ${_codec.decoder(value.withNullable(false))})';
    }
    if (isJson(value)) {
      return field.required ? 'r.requiredValue($key)' : 'r.optionalValue($key)';
    }
    final decoder = _codec.decoder(value.withNullable(false));
    if (!field.required) return 'r.optional($key, $decoder)';
    return value.nullable
        ? 'r.requiredNullable($key, $decoder)'
        : 'r.required($key, $decoder)';
  }
}
````

### Task 9: Fix from the round trip: an optional constant is written back only when the decoded JSON had it (Medusa)

**Files:**
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/CHANGELOG.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/model_constructors.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/model_emitter.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/fixtures/optional_constant.yaml`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/generated_code_test.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/model_library_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/optional_constant_test.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/CHANGELOG.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/lib/src/json/json_reader.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/test/json/json_reader_constant_test.dart`
- Write (by `dart run bin/corpus.dart update`): `packages/typed_openapi_tool/corpus.lock`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: task 8 (`ModelConstructors`); the runtime's `JsonReader.constant`.
- Produces: `bool JsonReader.constant(String key, Object value, {bool required = true})` (runtime: whether the constant was there; it was `void`); `ModelConstructors.absent` (`'_absent'`); a model with an optional constant has `final List<String> _absent`, a public constructor `: _absent = const []`, a private constructor `_(this._absent, {...})` that `fromJson` calls with `[if (!r.constant(key, value, required: false)) key, ...]`, `toJson` entries `if (!_absent.contains(key)) key: value`, and `_absent` in `==` and `hashCode`.

Medusa's search facets are a `oneOf` whose first branch has an optional constant `type: value` and whose other branches take any string `type`. Decision 8 of M8 says `toJson` always writes a constant, so `{"field": "text"}` (only the first branch) came back as `{"field": "text", "type": "value"}`, which three branches match: a `union: structural` `oneOf` then decodes it as unknown. The rule keeps decision 8 for what it was about and makes the round trip faithful: a required constant is always written; an optional one is written unless the decoded JSON did not have it. A model made in code has `_absent` empty and writes every constant, as before, so a request body still carries the constants a server dispatches on (OpenAI's optional `type: message` is one). The runtime's `JsonReader.constant` returns whether the constant was there (an explicit `null` reads as absent, as before). Output changes: every model with an optional constant gains the field, the private constructor and the conditional entry; no golden file has one, so no golden changes (the M8 test of the `Root` model with an optional `kind` changes two expected lines). In the corpus: 181 `stripe`, 24 `openai`, 2 `github` models (`raw`), and in `structural` also 5 `discord` and Medusa's facet; every round-trip failure is gone.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/fixtures/optional_constant.yaml`:

````yaml
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: Optional constants, version: "1"}
paths:
  /facets:
    get:
      operationId: getFacet
      responses:
        '200':
          description: ok
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Facet'}
components:
  schemas:
    # Medusa's search facets: a `oneOf` whose first branch has an optional
    # constant `type`, and whose others take any string `type`.
    Facet:
      oneOf:
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/ValueFacet'
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/RangeFacet'
    ValueFacet:
      type: object
      required: [field]
      properties:
        field: {type: string}
        type: {const: value}
        version: {const: 2}
    RangeFacet:
      type: object
      required: [field, type]
      properties:
        field: {type: string}
        type: {type: string}
        marker: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Marker'}
    Marker:
      type: object
      properties:
        kind: {const: marker}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/optional_constant_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/config/generation_config.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/overrides/override_set.dart';

import '../helpers/emit_fixture.dart';

void main() {
  late String model;
  late String marker;
  setUpAll(() {
    final files = emitFixture(
      'optional_constant.yaml',
      generation: const GenerationConfig(union: UnionMode.structural),
    );
    model = files['models/value_facet.dart']!;
    marker = files['models/marker.dart']!;
  });

  test('a model made in code writes its optional constants', () {
    expect(
      model,
      contains('const ValueFacet({required this.field}) : _absent = const [];'),
    );
    expect(model, contains("if (!_absent.contains('type')) 'type': 'value'"));
    expect(model, contains("if (!_absent.contains('version')) 'version': 2"));
  });

  test('fromJson remembers the optional constants that were absent', () {
    expect(
      model,
      contains('const ValueFacet._(this._absent, {required this.field});'),
    );
    expect(
      model,
      contains(
        "if (!r.constant('type', 'value', required: false)) 'type',",
      ),
    );
    expect(model, contains('return ValueFacet._('));
    expect(model, contains('final List<String> _absent;'));
  });

  test('a model of only an optional constant has both constructors', () {
    expect(marker, contains('const Marker() : _absent = const [];'));
    expect(marker, contains('const Marker._(this._absent);'));
    expect(marker, contains('return Marker._('));
  });

  test('the absent constants are part of equality', () {
    expect(
      model,
      contains('const DeepEquality().equals(other._absent, _absent)'),
    );
    expect(model, contains('hash([field, _absent])'));
  });
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/test/json/json_reader_constant_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_runtime/typed_openapi_runtime.dart';

void main() {
  test('a constant says whether it was there', () {
    const r = JsonReader({'kind': 'cat', 'tag': null}, schema: 'Cat');
    expect(r.constant('kind', 'cat'), isTrue);
    expect(r.constant('kind', 'cat', required: false), isTrue);
    expect(r.constant('tag', 'x', required: false), isFalse);
    expect(r.constant('gone', 'x', required: false), isFalse);
  });
}
````

````diff
 ## 0.0.1 - Unreleased
 
 ### Output changes
+- A model with an optional constant (a `const` or one-value `enum` the
+  schema does not require) remembers which of them the decoded JSON did
+  not have, in a private `_absent` list, and `toJson` leaves those out, so
+  a decoded value is written back as it came (Medusa's `union: structural`
+  `oneOf` of search facets no longer decodes its own JSON as unknown). A
+  model made in code still writes every constant. These models gain a
+  private constructor `_`, and `==`/`hashCode` compare `_absent`. No golden
+  file changes; in the corpus the models with optional constants of
+  `stripe`, `openai`, `github`, `discord` and `medusa` change (M11).
 - A union with a discriminator picks a variant whose JSON is not an object
   (an array, a string, a number, a boolean) by its JSON type before it
   reads the discriminator, so GitHub's `repos/get-content` decodes a
````

### Task 10: The escaping fuzz, `bin/fuzz.dart`

**Files:**
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/typed_openapi.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/bin/fuzz.dart`

- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/fuzz/fuzz_build.dart`

- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/fuzz/fuzz_run.dart`

- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/fuzz/fuzz_spec.dart`

- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/fuzz/leak_check.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/fuzz/literal_check.dart`

- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/fuzz/fuzz_run_test.dart`

- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/fuzz/spec_strings_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: task 3 (`CorpusPackage`, `PackageGate`); task 7 (a non-object variant of a discriminator union is picked by its JSON type); the generator's `ConfigLoader`, `SpecChecker`, `TextReporter`, `ClientIr` and its nodes, and `ApiLibrary` (exported here).
- Produces: `Hostile`, `FuzzSchemas`, `FuzzOperations`, `FuzzSpec`, `FuzzInput`, `FuzzBuild`, `FuzzOutput`, `LeakCheck`, `DartLiterals`, `LiteralCheck`, `SpecStrings`, `FuzzRun`, `FuzzReport`, `FuzzCli`; `export 'src/emit/api_library.dart' show ApiLibrary;`.

Spec decision 5. The M9 harness with its M10 event-stream extension moves in, split by responsibility: `FuzzSchemas` and `FuzzOperations` draw the schemas and operations, `FuzzSpec` a spec and an overrides file per seed (OpenAPI 3.0, 3.1 and 3.2; `sse` overrides on a third of the operations). `Hostile` holds the strings: names for the literal sinks (property keys, enum values, discriminator properties, parameter and operation names, event names: quotes, `$`, a backslash, `*/`, `{@macro}`, line breaks, a bidi control, U+2029, reserved and runtime names, a tab), texts for the doc sinks (descriptions, titles: each tries to start a line `injected();`), and media types valid by RFC 9110 that hold `'` and `$`. Each seed is one config of six clients (`{Tag}Calls` groups, by path with optional tri-state, one class with the extension; each in both `union` modes), built twice in-process (`SpecChecker`, `ApiLibrary`). The checks: (1) the two builds are equal, and every 12th seed's output (one client) goes into a sample package that passes `dart analyze --fatal-infos`; (2) `LiteralCheck` evaluates every string literal of each sampled client in the Dart VM and requires every string the IR keeps from the spec among the values; (3) `LeakCheck`: no line of code starts with smuggled code, no raw control (a tab is allowed: `DartCode` keeps it in doc spans), DEL, U+0085, U+2028, U+2029 or bidi control in the code, and none in the text report. A crash ends the run and names the seed. A variant of another JSON type (a string component in a discriminator union) is picked by its type since task 7, so its discriminator value is no sink: `SpecStrings` leaves it out (the first 2000-seed run on top of task 7 reported five such values). No seed found a defect of the generator (see "Fuzz"), so no fixture is added.

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/fuzz/dart_literals_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/fuzz/dart_literals.dart';

void main() {
  test('finds plain, raw and triple-quoted literals, not comments', () {
    const source = r"""
// 'in a comment'
/// 'in a doc comment'
/* 'in a block' */
const a = 'it\'s';
const b = r'raw\n';
const c = '''three
lines''';
const d = "double";
""";
    expect(DartLiterals(source).scan(), [
      r"'it\'s'",
      r"r'raw\n'",
      "'''three\nlines'''",
      '"double"',
    ]);
  });

  test('skips an interpolated literal and scans its interpolation', () {
    const source = r"""
final a = 'x $name';
final b = 'y ${m['key']} z';
final c = '\$cost';
""";
    expect(DartLiterals(source).scan(), ["'key'", r"'\$cost'"]);
  });
}
````

````dart
@TestOn('vm')
library;

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/fuzz/fuzz_run.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/fuzz/fuzz_spec.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/fuzz/literal_check.dart';

import '../helpers/temp_dir.dart';

void main() {
  test('a seed always gives the same spec, and hostile strings', () {
    const spec = FuzzSpec();
    expect(spec.of(7).spec, spec.of(7).spec);
    expect(spec.of(7).spec, isNot(spec.of(8).spec));
    final all = [for (var seed = 0; seed < 20; seed++) spec.of(seed).spec];
    for (final name in ["it's", r'$cost', '*/ name']) {
      expect(all.join(), contains(name), reason: name);
    }
  });

  test('a few seeds build, analyze and keep their literals', () async {
    final temp = TempDir();
    final log = StringBuffer();
    final report = await FuzzRun(
      work: p.join(temp.path, 'fuzz'),
      runtime: p.normalize(p.absolute(p.join('..', 'typed_openapi_runtime'))),
      log: log,
      sampleEvery: 2,
    ).run(from: 0, count: 4);
    expect(report.failures, isEmpty);
    expect(report.builds, greaterThan(0));
    expect(report.sampled, greaterThan(0));
    expect('$log', isEmpty);
  }, timeout: const Timeout(Duration(minutes: 3)));

  test('a spec string that no literal holds is reported', () async {
    final temp = TempDir()
      ..write('pubspec.yaml', 'name: sample\nenvironment:\n  sdk: ^3.10.0\n')
      ..write('lib/s1/a.dart', "const a = 'it\\'s';\n");
    final failures = await LiteralCheck(temp.path).run({
      's1': ["it's", r'$cost'],
    });
    expect(failures, [r's1: the spec string "$cost" is in no literal.']);
  }, timeout: const Timeout(Duration(minutes: 1)));
}
````

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/fuzz/leak_check.dart';

void main() {
  const check = LeakCheck();

  test('a line that starts with smuggled code is a leak', () {
    expect(check.code('c', {'a.dart': '/// doc */\ninjected();\n'}), [
      'c/a.dart: spec text left its sink: injected();',
    ]);
  });

  test('a raw bidi control or line separator in code is a leak', () {
    expect(check.code('c', {'a.dart': "const a = '\u202E';\n"}), [
      'c/a.dart: a raw U+202E in the code.',
    ]);
    expect(check.code('c', {'a.dart': '/// a\u2028b\n'}), [
      'c/a.dart: a raw U+2028 in the code.',
    ]);
  });

  test('a tab in a doc comment is allowed', () {
    expect(check.code('c', {'a.dart': '/// `tab\there`\n'}), isEmpty);
  });

  test('a raw control character in the report is a leak', () {
    expect(check.report('error - a\x1bb'), [
      'The report holds a raw U+001B.',
    ]);
    expect(check.report('error - a\nb\n'), isEmpty);
  });
}
````

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';

import '../helpers/client_ir.dart';

const _spec = r'''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
paths:
  /pets:
    get:
      operationId: getPet
      parameters:
        - {name: "it's", in: query, schema: {type: string}}
      responses:
        "200":
          description: ok
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: {$ref: "#/components/schemas/Pet"}
components:
  schemas:
    Pet:
      oneOf:
        - $ref: "#/components/schemas/Cat"
        - $ref: "#/components/schemas/Name"
      discriminator: {propertyName: kind}
    Cat: {type: object, properties: {kind: {type: string}, $cost: {type: integer}}}
    Name: {type: string}
''';

void main() {
  test('holds keys, names and the values of object variants only', () {
    final strings = const SpecStrings().of(clientIr(_spec));
    expect(strings, containsAll([r'$cost', "it's", 'kind', 'Cat']));
    // A string variant is picked by its JSON type, not by a value.
    expect(strings, isNot(contains('Name')));
  });
}
````
