**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/fuzz/dart_literals.dart`:

````dart
/// Finds the string literals of Dart source that the fuzz evaluates: every
/// single or double quoted literal, raw or not, one or three quotes, that
/// has no interpolation. Comments are skipped; an interpolation `${...}` is
/// scanned as code, so literals inside it are found too.
///
/// The source is generated code that passes the analyzer, so the scanner
/// needs no error recovery.
final class DartLiterals {
  /// Creates the scanner of [_source].
  DartLiterals(this._source);

  final String _source;
  var _at = 0;
  final _found = <String>[];

  /// The text of each literal without interpolation, in source order.
  List<String> scan() {
    _code(untilBrace: false);
    return _found;
  }

  void _code({required bool untilBrace}) {
    var depth = 0;
    while (_at < _source.length) {
      final char = _source[_at];
      if (_source.startsWith('//', _at)) {
        final end = _source.indexOf('\n', _at);
        _at = end < 0 ? _source.length : end;
      } else if (_source.startsWith('/*', _at)) {
        _at = _source.indexOf('*/', _at) + 2;
      } else if (char == "'" || char == '"') {
        _string(raw: _at > 0 && _source[_at - 1] == 'r' && _isRawPrefix());
      } else {
        _at++;
        if (char == '{') depth++;
        if (char != '}') continue;
        if (depth == 0 && untilBrace) return;
        depth--;
      }
    }
  }

  bool _isRawPrefix() =>
      _at < 2 || !RegExp(r'[\w$]').hasMatch(_source[_at - 2]);

  void _string({required bool raw}) {
    final start = raw ? _at - 1 : _at;
    final quote =
        _source.startsWith("'''", _at) || _source.startsWith('"""', _at)
        ? _source.substring(_at, _at + 3)
        : _source[_at];
    _at += quote.length;
    var interpolated = false;
    while (!_source.startsWith(quote, _at)) {
      final char = _source[_at];
      if (!raw && char == r'\') {
        _at += 2;
      } else if (!raw && char == r'$') {
        interpolated = true;
        _at++;
        if (_source[_at] == '{') {
          _at++;
          _code(untilBrace: true);
        }
      } else {
        _at++;
      }
    }
    _at += quote.length;
    if (!interpolated) _found.add(_source.substring(start, _at));
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/fuzz/fuzz_build.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:typed_openapi/typed_openapi.dart';

import 'fuzz_spec.dart';

/// What one build of a fuzz seed gave: the files of each client, the IR of
/// each client, and the text report of the run.
typedef FuzzOutput = ({
  Map<String, Map<String, String>> files,
  Map<String, ClientIr> irs,
  String report,
});

/// Builds the spec of one seed as `generate` would, in-process: one client
/// per generation config and `union` mode, all of the same spec and the
/// same overrides file.
final class FuzzBuild {
  /// Creates the build of the seed's [input] in the empty directory
  /// [directory].
  FuzzBuild(this.directory, FuzzInput input) {
    File(p.join(directory, 'spec.json')).writeAsStringSync(input.spec);
    File(
      p.join(directory, 'overrides.json'),
    ).writeAsStringSync(input.overrides);
    final clients = StringBuffer();
    for (final (name, settings) in configs) {
      for (final mode in const ['raw', 'structural']) {
        clients.writeln(
          '  ${name}_$mode: {input: spec.json, output: out/${name}_$mode, '
          'overrides: overrides.json, union: $mode, $settings}',
        );
      }
    }
    File(config).writeAsStringSync('clients:\n$clients');
  }

  /// The generation configs, by name: grouping by tag with a template, by
  /// path with optional tri-state fields, one class with the extension.
  static const configs = [
    ('tag', "naming: {group: '{Tag}Calls'}"),
    ('path', 'group_by: path, tri_state: optional'),
    ('single', 'group_by: single, tri_state: extension'),
  ];

  /// The directory of the seed's files.
  final String directory;

  /// The config of the build.
  String get config => p.join(directory, 'typed_openapi.yaml');

  /// Checks and emits every client; a crash of the generator is not
  /// caught.
  FuzzOutput build() {
    final bag = DiagnosticBag();
    final project = ConfigLoader(bag).load(config);
    final files = <String, Map<String, String>>{};
    final irs = <String, ClientIr>{};
    for (final check
        in project == null
            ? const <ClientCheck>[]
            : SpecChecker(bag).check(project)) {
      if (check.ir case final ir?) {
        irs[check.client.name] = ir;
        files[check.client.name] = const ApiLibrary().files(
          ir,
          client: 'api',
        );
      }
    }
    return (files: files, irs: irs, report: const TextReporter().format(bag));
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/fuzz/fuzz_cli.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:args/args.dart';
import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

import 'fuzz_run.dart';

/// The `fuzz` command line of the repository (M11): runs the escaping fuzz
/// on a range of seeds and exits `1` when it finds a problem.
final class FuzzCli {
  /// Creates the command line; the summary goes to [out], problems to [err].
  FuzzCli({StringSink? out, StringSink? err})
    : _out = out ?? stdout,
      _err = err ?? stderr;

  final StringSink _out;
  final StringSink _err;

  static final _parser = ArgParser()
    ..addOption('seeds', defaultsTo: '200', help: 'How many seeds to run.')
    ..addOption('from', defaultsTo: '0', help: 'The first seed.')
    ..addOption(
      'sample-every',
      defaultsTo: '12',
      help: 'Every how many seeds an output is analyzed.',
    )
    ..addOption(
      'work',
      defaultsTo: '.dart_tool/fuzz',
      help: 'Where the sample package is written.',
    )
    ..addOption(
      'runtime',
      defaultsTo: '../typed_openapi_runtime',
      help: 'The typed_openapi_runtime package the sample depends on.',
    );

  /// Runs the fuzz with [args]; returns the exit code.
  Future<int> run(List<String> args) async {
    final ArgResults results;
    try {
      results = _parser.parse(args);
    } on FormatException catch (e) {
      _err
        ..writeln(e.message)
        ..writeln(_parser.usage);
      return 64;
    }
    int number(String name) => int.parse(results.option(name)!);
    final report = await FuzzRun(
      work: p.normalize(p.absolute(results.option('work')!)),
      runtime: p.normalize(p.absolute(results.option('runtime')!)),
      log: _err,
      sampleEvery: number('sample-every'),
    ).run(from: number('from'), count: number('seeds'));
    report.failures.forEach(_err.writeln);
    _out.writeln(
      '${number('seeds')} seeds: ${report.builds} builds, '
      '${report.files} files, ${report.sampled} sampled; '
      '${report.failures.length} problems.',
    );
    return report.failures.isEmpty ? 0 : 1;
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/fuzz/fuzz_operations.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:math';

import 'fuzz_schemas.dart';
import 'hostile.dart';

/// Random operations of one fuzz spec: parameters of every location and
/// style, bodies and responses of every media type the generator knows,
/// event streams, and `sse` overrides (from the M9 and M10 harnesses).
final class FuzzOperations {
  /// Creates the operations drawn from [_random] over [schemas].
  FuzzOperations(this._random, this.schemas);

  final Random _random;

  /// The schemas the operations refer to.
  final FuzzSchemas schemas;

  T _pick<T>(List<T> items) => schemas.pick(items);

  /// A parameter; [i] keeps names of one operation apart.
  Map<String, Object?> parameter(int i) =>
      _pick<Map<String, Object?> Function()>([
        () => {'name': 'q$i', 'in': 'query', 'schema': schemas.primitive()},
        () => {
          'name': _pick(Hostile.names),
          'in': 'query',
          'description': _pick(Hostile.texts),
          'schema': schemas.primitive(),
        },
        () => {
          'name': 'h$i',
          'in': 'header',
          'required': true,
          'schema': schemas.primitive(),
        },
        () => {
          'name': 'c$i',
          'in': 'cookie',
          'explode': schemas.coin(),
          'schema': schemas.primitive(),
        },
        () => {
          'name': 'd$i',
          'in': 'query',
          'style': 'deepObject',
          'explode': true,
          'schema': {
            'type': 'object',
            'additionalProperties': {'type': 'string'},
          },
        },
        () => {
          'name': 'p$i',
          'in': 'query',
          'style': _pick(['pipeDelimited', 'spaceDelimited', 'form']),
          'schema': {'type': 'array', 'items': schemas.enumeration()},
        },
        () => {
          'name': 'j$i',
          'in': 'query',
          'content': {
            'application/json': {'schema': schemas.primitive()},
          },
        },
        () => {
          'name': "x'\$\n*/$i",
          'in': 'query',
          'schema': {'type': 'string', 'default': r"*/ $x '"},
        },
      ])();

  /// The content of a body or a response.
  Map<String, Object?> content() => _pick<Map<String, Object?> Function()>([
    () => {
      'application/json': {'schema': schemas.ref()},
    },
    () => {
      'application/json': {'schema': schemas.variant()},
    },
    () => {
      _pick(Hostile.mediaTypes): {'schema': schemas.ref()},
    },
    () => {
      'text/plain': {
        'schema': {'type': 'string'},
      },
    },
    () => {
      'application/octet-stream': {
        'schema': {'type': 'string', 'format': 'binary'},
      },
    },
    () => {
      'application/x-www-form-urlencoded': {
        'schema': schemas.inlineObject(),
      },
    },
    () => {
      'multipart/form-data': {
        'schema': {
          'type': 'object',
          'properties': {
            'f': {'type': 'string', 'format': 'binary'},
            'm': schemas.primitive(),
          },
        },
      },
    },
    () => {'text/event-stream': _stream()},
    () => {
      'application/json': {'schema': schemas.ref()},
      'text/event-stream': _stream(),
    },
  ])();

  Map<String, Object?> _stream() => _pick<Map<String, Object?> Function()>([
    () => {'schema': schemas.primitive()},
    () => <String, Object?>{},
    () => {
      'itemSchema': {
        'oneOf': [
          for (var i = 0; i < 1 + _random.nextInt(4); i++)
            {
              'properties': {
                'event': {'const': _pick(Hostile.names)},
                if (schemas.coin())
                  'data': {
                    'type': 'string',
                    'contentMediaType': 'application/json',
                    'contentSchema': schemas.ref(),
                  },
              },
            },
        ],
      },
    },
  ])();

  /// The responses of an operation.
  Map<String, Object?> responses() => {
    for (var k = 0; k < 1 + _random.nextInt(4); k++)
      _pick([
        '200',
        '201',
        '204',
        '2XX',
        '400',
        '404',
        '4XX',
        '500',
        'default',
      ]): {
        'description': _pick(Hostile.texts),
        if (schemas.coin()) 'content': content(),
      },
  };

  /// An `sse` override of an operation.
  Map<String, Object?> sseOverride() => {
    if (schemas.coin())
      'events': {
        for (var i = 0; i < 1 + _random.nextInt(3); i++)
          _pick(Hostile.names): _pick<Object?>([null, _pick(schemas.names)]),
      },
    if (schemas.coin()) 'unknown': _pick(['keep', 'ignore']),
    if (schemas.coin()) 'method': _pick(['stream', 'only', 'none']),
  };
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/fuzz/fuzz_run.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

import '../corpus/corpus_package.dart';
import '../corpus/package_gate.dart';
import 'fuzz_build.dart';
import 'fuzz_spec.dart';
import 'leak_check.dart';
import 'literal_check.dart';
import 'spec_strings.dart';

/// What a fuzz run found.
typedef FuzzReport = ({
  int builds,
  int files,
  int sampled,
  List<String> failures,
});

/// The escaping fuzz (spec decision 5): builds the spec of each seed twice
/// in every generation config and `union` mode, and checks that
/// - the two builds give the same files (determinism);
/// - spec text stays in its sink, in the code and in the report
///   ([LeakCheck]);
/// - a sample of the outputs (every [sampleEvery]-th seed) passes
///   `dart analyze --fatal-infos` ([PackageGate]) and holds every spec
///   string as a literal of the same value ([LiteralCheck]).
///
/// A crash of the generator is not caught: it ends the run, and [log] names
/// the seed.
final class FuzzRun {
  /// Creates the run that writes its sample package into [work] (absolute)
  /// with the runtime in [runtime].
  const FuzzRun({
    required this.work,
    required this.runtime,
    required this.log,
    this.sampleEvery = 12,
  });

  /// The directory of the sample package.
  final String work;

  /// The directory of `typed_openapi_runtime`.
  final String runtime;

  /// Where the run names the seed it is on when the generator crashes.
  final StringSink log;

  /// Every how many seeds an output goes into the sample.
  final int sampleEvery;

  /// Runs the seeds `from` to `from + count - 1`.
  Future<FuzzReport> run({required int from, required int count}) async {
    final sample = CorpusPackage(work, 'fuzz')
      ..clean()
      ..write(const {}, runtime);
    final temp = Directory.systemTemp.createTempSync('typed_openapi_fuzz_');
    final failures = <String>[];
    final strings = <String, List<String>>{};
    var builds = 0;
    var files = 0;
    try {
      for (var seed = from; seed < from + count; seed++) {
        final seen = _seed(seed, p.join(temp.path, '$seed'), sample);
        builds += seen.builds;
        files += seen.files;
        failures.addAll(seen.failures);
        if (seen.strings case final found?) strings['s$seed'] = found;
      }
    } finally {
      temp.deleteSync(recursive: true);
    }
    if (strings.isNotEmpty) {
      failures
        ..addAll(await PackageGate(work).run())
        ..addAll(await LiteralCheck(work).run(strings));
    }
    return (
      builds: builds,
      files: files,
      sampled: strings.length,
      failures: failures,
    );
  }

  /// Builds the seed [seed] twice in [directory], checks the builds, and
  /// writes the sampled output into [sample].
  ({int builds, int files, List<String> failures, List<String>? strings}) _seed(
    int seed,
    String directory,
    CorpusPackage sample,
  ) {
    Directory(directory).createSync(recursive: true);
    final build = FuzzBuild(directory, const FuzzSpec().of(seed));
    FuzzOutput? first;
    try {
      first = build.build();
    } finally {
      if (first == null) log.writeln('The generator crashed on seed $seed.');
    }
    final second = build.build();
    return (
      builds: first.files.length,
      files: first.files.values.fold(0, (n, f) => n + f.length),
      failures: _problems(seed, first, second),
      strings: seed % sampleEvery == 0 ? _sample(seed, first, sample) : null,
    );
  }

  static List<String> _problems(int seed, FuzzOutput first, FuzzOutput second) {
    const leaks = LeakCheck();
    return [
      for (final MapEntry(key: client, value: files)
          in first.files.entries) ...[
        if (!_same(files, second.files[client]))
          'seed $seed: $client is not deterministic.',
        ...leaks.code('seed $seed $client', files),
      ],
      for (final problem in leaks.report(first.report)) 'seed $seed: $problem',
    ];
  }

  /// Writes the output of one client of the seed into [sample] and returns
  /// its spec strings; null when the client has no IR.
  static List<String>? _sample(
    int seed,
    FuzzOutput output,
    CorpusPackage sample,
  ) {
    final client = '${FuzzBuild.configs[seed % 3].$1}_raw';
    final ir = output.irs[client];
    if (ir == null) return null;
    for (final MapEntry(key: path, value: text)
        in (output.files[client] ?? const {}).entries) {
      File(p.join(sample.outputOf('s$seed'), path))
        ..createSync(recursive: true)
        ..writeAsStringSync(text);
    }
    return const SpecStrings().of(ir);
  }

  static bool _same(Map<String, String> a, Map<String, String>? b) =>
      b != null &&
      a.length == b.length &&
      a.entries.every((e) => b[e.key] == e.value);
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/fuzz/fuzz_schemas.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:math';

import 'hostile.dart';

/// Random schemas of one fuzz spec: primitives with constraints and formats,
/// enums and constants, unions, `allOf`, maps and references between the
/// components [names], with hostile property names, enum values and
/// descriptions.
final class FuzzSchemas {
  /// Creates the schemas drawn from [_random] that refer to [names].
  FuzzSchemas(this._random, this.names);

  final Random _random;

  /// The names of the components, sorted.
  final List<String> names;

  /// One of [items], drawn at random.
  T pick<T>(List<T> items) => items[_random.nextInt(items.length)];

  /// A random boolean.
  bool coin() => _random.nextBool();

  /// A reference to a random component.
  Map<String, Object?> ref() => {
    r'$ref': '#/components/schemas/${pick(names)}',
  };

  /// An inline object of one to three properties, some named with hostile
  /// text.
  Map<String, Object?> inlineObject() => {
    'type': 'object',
    'description': pick(Hostile.texts),
    'properties': {
      for (var i = 0; i < 1 + _random.nextInt(3); i++)
        coin() ? 'q$i' : pick(Hostile.names): primitive(),
    },
  };

  /// An enum or a constant.
  Map<String, Object?> enumeration() => pick<Map<String, Object?> Function()>([
    () => {
      'type': 'string',
      'enum': ['a', 'b'],
    },
    () => {
      'type': 'string',
      'enum': [pick(Hostile.names), pick(Hostile.names)],
    },
    () => {'const': pick(Hostile.names)},
    () => {
      'type': 'integer',
      'enum': [1, 2],
    },
    () => {
      'type': 'integer',
      'enum': [7],
    },
    () => {'const': true},
    () => {'const': 1.5},
    () => {'const': null},
  ])();

  /// A property schema.
  Map<String, Object?> primitive() => pick<Map<String, Object?> Function()>([
    () => {
      'type': 'string',
      'minLength': _random.nextInt(3),
      'maxLength': 9,
      'pattern': pick(['^a', r"^[*/'$\\]"]),
    },
    () => {'type': 'integer', 'minimum': 0, 'exclusiveMaximum': 10},
    () => {'type': 'number', 'minimum': 0, 'multipleOf': 0.5},
    () => {
      'type': 'array',
      'items': {'type': 'string'},
      'minItems': 1,
    },
    enumeration,
    () => {'type': 'string', 'format': 'date-time'},
    () => {'type': 'string', 'format': 'date'},
    () => {'type': 'string', 'format': 'byte'},
    () => {'type': 'integer', 'format': 'int64'},
    () => {'type': 'boolean', 'default': true},
    () => <String, Object?>{'description': pick(Hostile.texts)},
    () => {
      'type': 'object',
      'additionalProperties': {'type': 'string'},
    },
    ref,
    () => {
      'allOf': [
        ref(),
        {'description': pick(Hostile.texts), 'maxLength': 3},
      ],
    },
    () => {
      'anyOf': [
        {'type': 'string', 'maxLength': 5000},
        {
          'enum': [''],
        },
      ],
    },
    () => {
      'oneOf': [
        {'type': 'string'},
        {
          'enum': [pick(Hostile.names), 'c*/d'],
        },
        {'type': 'integer'},
      ],
    },
    () => {
      'anyOf': [
        {'type': 'integer'},
        {'type': 'null'},
      ],
    },
  ])();

  /// A branch of a union.
  Map<String, Object?> variant() => pick<Map<String, Object?> Function()>([
    ref,
    ref,
    inlineObject,
    () => {'type': 'string'},
    () => {'type': 'integer'},
    () => {'type': 'number'},
    () => {'type': 'boolean'},
    () => {
      'type': 'array',
      'items': {'type': 'string'},
    },
    () => {
      'type': 'object',
      'additionalProperties': {'type': 'string'},
    },
    () => {'type': 'string', 'format': 'date'},
    enumeration,
    enumeration,
    () => {
      'type': ['string', 'null'],
    },
  ])();

  /// A component schema.
  Map<String, Object?> component() => pick<Map<String, Object?> Function()>([
    () => {
      'type': 'object',
      'description': pick(Hostile.texts),
      'required': [pick(Hostile.names)],
      'properties': {
        for (var i = 0; i < 1 + _random.nextInt(4); i++)
          pick(['q$i', 'kind', ...Hostile.names]): primitive(),
      },
      if (coin())
        'additionalProperties': pick([
          {'type': 'integer'},
          ref(),
        ]),
    },
    () => {
      'oneOf': [for (var i = 0; i < 2 + _random.nextInt(3); i++) variant()],
    },
    () => {
      'anyOf': [for (var i = 0; i < 2 + _random.nextInt(3); i++) variant()],
      if (coin()) 'discriminator': {'propertyName': pick(Hostile.names)},
    },
    () => {
      'type': 'string',
      'enum': [
        for (final name in Hostile.names)
          if (coin()) name,
      ],
    },
    () => {
      'type': 'integer',
      'enum': [1, 2],
    },
    () => {
      'allOf': [ref(), inlineObject()],
    },
    () => {'type': 'string', 'description': pick(Hostile.texts)},
  ])();
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/fuzz/fuzz_spec.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:math';

import 'fuzz_operations.dart';
import 'fuzz_schemas.dart';
import 'hostile.dart';

/// The spec and the overrides file the fuzz builds from one seed.
typedef FuzzInput = ({String spec, String overrides});

/// Builds a random spec from a seed: the same seed gives the same spec.
final class FuzzSpec {
  /// Creates the builder.
  const FuzzSpec();

  static const _stems = ['Pet', 'Cat', 'Status', 'Kind', 'Id', 'Get0'];
  static const _suffixes = [
    '',
    'Response',
    'Error',
    'Request',
    'Unknown',
    'Dio',
  ];
  static const _methods = ['get', 'post', 'patch', 'put', 'delete'];
  static const _id = {
    'name': 'id',
    'in': 'path',
    'required': true,
    'schema': {'type': 'string'},
  };

  /// The spec (JSON) and the overrides file (JSON) of [seed].
  FuzzInput of(int seed) {
    final random = Random(seed);
    final schemas = FuzzSchemas(random, _names(random));
    final operations = FuzzOperations(random, schemas);
    final overrides = <String, Object?>{};
    final paths = <String, Object?>{
      for (var i = 0; i < 1 + random.nextInt(3); i++)
        '/p$i/{id}': _pathItem(i, random, operations, overrides),
    };
    return (
      spec: jsonEncode({
        'openapi': schemas.pick(['3.0.3', '3.1.0', '3.2.0']),
        'info': {'title': schemas.pick(Hostile.texts), 'version': '1'},
        'paths': paths,
        'components': {
          'schemas': {
            for (final name in schemas.names) name: schemas.component(),
          },
        },
      }),
      overrides: jsonEncode({'operations': overrides}),
    );
  }

  static List<String> _names(Random random) {
    final names = <String>{};
    final count = 3 + random.nextInt(8);
    while (names.length < count) {
      names.add(
        '${_stems[random.nextInt(_stems.length)]}'
        '${_suffixes[random.nextInt(_suffixes.length)]}',
      );
    }
    return names.toList()..sort();
  }

  /// The path item `/p<i>/{id}`; an operation's `sse` override goes into
  /// [overrides].
  static Map<String, Object?> _pathItem(
    int i,
    Random random,
    FuzzOperations operations,
    Map<String, Object?> overrides,
  ) {
    final schemas = operations.schemas;
    final item = <String, Object?>{};
    for (final method in _methods) {
      if (random.nextBool()) continue;
      final id = schemas.pick(['$method$i', '$method$i', ...Hostile.names]);
      item[method] = {
        'operationId': id,
        'description': schemas.pick(Hostile.texts),
        if (schemas.coin())
          'tags': [
            schemas.pick(['t', 'Pet', ...Hostile.names]),
          ],
        'parameters': [
          for (var k = 0; k < random.nextInt(4); k++) operations.parameter(k),
        ],
        if (method != 'get' && schemas.coin())
          'requestBody': {'content': operations.content()},
        'responses': operations.responses(),
      };
      if (random.nextInt(3) == 0) {
        overrides[id] = {'sse': operations.sseOverride()};
      }
    }
    return {
      ...item,
      'parameters': [_id],
    };
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/fuzz/hostile.dart`:

````dart
/// The hostile strings the fuzz puts at every sink a spec string reaches
/// (spec decision 5).
abstract final class Hostile {
  /// Strings for sinks that become literals or names: property keys, enum
  /// values, parameter and event names, discriminator values. Quotes, `$`,
  /// backslashes, comment and doc-comment markers, line breaks, a bidi
  /// control, reserved words and names the runtime or the emitter use.
  static const List<String> names = [
    "it's",
    r'$cost',
    r'back\slash',
    '*/ name',
    '{@macro x}',
    'line\nbreak',
    'line\r\nfeed',
    '\u202Eevil',
    'para\u2029graph',
    'class',
    'toString',
    'hashCode',
    'unknown',
    '',
    'Delta',
    'delta',
    '"double"',
    'tab\there',
  ];

  /// Strings for sinks that become documentation: descriptions, titles and
  /// summaries; each tries to leave its comment.
  static const List<String> texts = [
    '*/ injected();',
    '\ninjected();',
    '\r\ninjected();',
    'a [link] and <b>tag</b> {@macro y}',
    '```\ncode\n```',
    '\u2028injected();',
    r'ends with a backslash \',
    r"$it's",
  ];

  /// Media types that are valid by RFC 9110 and hold characters that a
  /// Dart string literal must escape.
  static const List<String> mediaTypes = [
    r"application/x-it's$cost+json",
    'application/vnd.a+json',
  ];
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/fuzz/leak_check.dart`:

````dart
/// Checks that spec text stays in its sink (spec decision 5, check 3): no
/// line of generated code starts with text a spec string smuggled out of
/// its comment or literal, the code holds no raw line break other than
/// `\n` and no bidirectional control, and the report holds no raw control
/// character.
final class LeakCheck {
  /// Creates the check.
  const LeakCheck();

  /// Characters that must never be raw in code: C0 controls but `\t` and `\n`, DEL,
  /// U+0085, U+2028, U+2029 and the bidirectional controls.
  static final _unsafeInCode = RegExp(
    r'[\x00-\x08\x0b-\x1f\x7f\u0085\u2028\u2029'
    r'\u202a-\u202e\u2066-\u2069\u200e\u200f\u061c]',
  );

  /// A line that the fuzz's hostile texts would start after an escape.
  static final _smuggled = RegExp(r'^\s*(?:injected|void injected)');

  /// The problems of the files [files] of the client [client].
  List<String> code(String client, Map<String, String> files) => [
    for (final MapEntry(key: path, value: text) in files.entries) ...[
      if (_unsafeInCode.firstMatch(text) case final m?)
        '$client/$path: a raw U+${_hex(m[0] ?? '')} in the code.',
      for (final line in text.split('\n'))
        if (_smuggled.hasMatch(line))
          '$client/$path: spec text left its sink: $line',
    ],
  ];

  /// The problems of the text report [report].
  List<String> report(String report) => [
    if (_unsafeInCode.firstMatch(report) case final m?)
      'The report holds a raw U+${_hex(m[0] ?? '')}.',
  ];

  static String _hex(String char) =>
      char.codeUnitAt(0).toRadixString(16).toUpperCase().padLeft(4, '0');
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/fuzz/literal_check.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

import 'dart_literals.dart';

/// Checks that escaping never changes content (spec decision 5, check 2):
/// the string literals of each sampled client are evaluated by the Dart VM,
/// and every spec string the client's IR holds must be among their values.
final class LiteralCheck {
  /// Creates the check of the sample package in [directory], whose clients
  /// are `lib/<name>/`.
  const LiteralCheck(this.directory);

  /// The directory of the sample package.
  final String directory;

  /// The failures for [expected], the spec strings of each sampled client
  /// by its name; empty when every string is a literal's value.
  Future<List<String>> run(Map<String, List<String>> expected) async {
    final program = StringBuffer()
      ..writeln("import 'dart:convert';")
      ..writeln('const literals = <String, List<String>>{');
    for (final name in expected.keys) {
      program.writeln("  '$name': [");
      for (final literal in _literals(p.join(directory, 'lib', name))) {
        program.writeln('    $literal,');
      }
      program.writeln('  ],');
    }
    program
      ..writeln('};')
      ..writeln('void main() => print(jsonEncode(literals));');
    final file = p.join(directory, 'bin', 'literals.dart');
    File(file)
      ..createSync(recursive: true)
      ..writeAsStringSync('$program');
    final result = await Process.run(Platform.resolvedExecutable, [
      'run',
      file,
    ], workingDirectory: directory);
    if (result.exitCode != 0) {
      return ['The literals program failed: ${result.stderr}'];
    }
    final values = jsonDecode('${result.stdout}') as Map<String, Object?>;
    return [
      for (final MapEntry(key: name, value: strings) in expected.entries)
        for (final string in strings)
          if (!(values[name] as List<Object?>).contains(string))
            '$name: the spec string ${jsonEncode(string)} is in no literal.',
    ];
  }

  /// The literals of every Dart file under [folder], each once, sorted.
  static List<String> _literals(String folder) {
    final literals = <String>{};
    for (final entity in Directory(folder).listSync(recursive: true)) {
      if (entity is File && entity.path.endsWith('.dart')) {
        literals.addAll(DartLiterals(entity.readAsStringSync()).scan());
      }
    }
    return literals.toList()..sort();
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/fuzz/spec_strings.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:typed_openapi/typed_openapi.dart';

/// The spec strings a client's code must hold as string literals with the
/// same value: property keys, string enum values, discriminator properties
/// and the values of the variants an object is (a variant of another JSON
/// type is picked by that type), parameter names and event names, read
/// from the IR (which keeps them as the spec writes them).
final class SpecStrings {
  /// Creates the collector.
  const SpecStrings();

  /// The strings of [ir], sorted.
  List<String> of(ClientIr ir) {
    final byId = {for (final d in ir.declarations) d.id: d};
    bool isObject(DartType type) => switch (type) {
      MapType() || CoreType(kind: CoreKind.json) => true,
      NamedType(:final id) => byId[id] is ModelDecl || byId[id] is UnionDecl,
      _ => false,
    };
    final strings = <String>{
      for (final declaration in ir.declarations)
        ...switch (declaration) {
          ModelDecl(:final fields) => [for (final f in fields) f.jsonKey],
          EnumDecl(:final values) => [
            for (final v in values)
              if (v.value case final String text) text,
          ],
          UnionDecl(:final discriminator, :final variants) => [
            ?discriminator,
            if (discriminator != null)
              for (final v in variants)
                if (isObject(v.type)) ...v.discriminatorValues,
          ],
        },
      for (final group in ir.groups)
        for (final operation in group.operations) ...[
          for (final p in operation.parameters) p.wireName,
          for (final v
              in operation.events?.union?.variants ?? const <EventVariantIr>[])
            v.event,
        ],
    };
    return strings.toList()..sort();
  }
}
````

### Task 11: Semver: an output change needs a CHANGELOG entry and a raised version (R15.4)

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/corpus/corpus_cli.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/semver/semver_command.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/semver/semver_rule.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/semver/semver_rule_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: task 2 (`CorpusCli`); `git` on the path.
- Produces: `SemverRule.problems`, `SemverRule.heading` (`### Output changes`), `SemverCommand` (`corpus semver --base <rev> [--root <dir>]`).

Spec decision 7. `corpus semver` asks `git diff --name-only <base>...HEAD` whether `corpus.lock` or a file under `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/goldens` changed. The lock counts only once the base has one: M11 adds it, so its own pull request is judged by the goldens alone (it changes none, and its CHANGELOG has the "Output changes" of tasks 5, 7 and 9). When the output changed, `SemverRule` needs both an entry under `### Output changes` in the top `## ` section of `packages/typed_openapi/CHANGELOG.md` (which must differ from the base's) and a raised version (before 1.0 a higher minor or major; after it a higher major); each missing piece is one sentence that says what to add. The rule is tested without git; the command is a thin shell around it.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/semver/semver_rule_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_tool/src/semver/semver_rule.dart';

const _base = '## 0.1.0 - Unreleased\n\n### Added\n- Things.\n';
const _entry =
    '## 0.2.0 - Unreleased\n\n### Output changes\n'
    '- stripe: models/a.dart.\n\n## 0.1.0\n';

void main() {
  const rule = SemverRule();

  List<String> problems({
    bool changed = true,
    String version = '0.2.0',
    String changelog = _entry,
    String baseVersion = '0.1.0',
  }) => rule.problems(
    outputChanged: changed,
    basePubspec: 'name: typed_openapi\nversion: $baseVersion\n',
    pubspec: 'name: typed_openapi\nversion: $version\n',
    baseChangelog: _base,
    changelog: changelog,
  );

  test('no change of output needs nothing', () {
    expect(
      problems(changed: false, version: '0.1.0', changelog: _base),
      isEmpty,
    );
  });

  test('a change with an entry and a raised minor version complies', () {
    expect(problems(), isEmpty);
  });

  test('a change without an entry says what to add', () {
    expect(problems(changelog: _base), [
      contains('add an entry under "### Output changes"'),
    ]);
  });

  test('before 1.0 the minor version must rise, after it the major', () {
    expect(problems(version: '0.1.1'), [
      contains('from 0.1.0 to 0.2.0 or later (minor before 1.0)'),
    ]);
    expect(problems(baseVersion: '1.2.0', version: '1.3.0'), [
      contains('from 1.2.0 to 2.0.0 or later (major after 1.0)'),
    ]);
    expect(problems(baseVersion: '1.2.0', version: '2.0.0'), isEmpty);
  });
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/semver/semver_command.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:args/command_runner.dart';

import 'semver_rule.dart';

/// `corpus semver`: compares the change with its base branch and fails when
/// the generated output changed without a CHANGELOG entry under "Output
/// changes" and a raised version (R15.4, [SemverRule]).
final class SemverCommand extends Command<int> {
  /// Creates the command; lines go to [_out], problems to [_err].
  SemverCommand(this._out, this._err) {
    argParser
      ..addOption(
        'base',
        defaultsTo: 'origin/main',
        help: 'The git revision the change is compared with.',
      )
      ..addOption(
        'root',
        defaultsTo: '../..',
        help: 'The root of the repository.',
      );
  }

  final StringSink _out;
  final StringSink _err;

  /// The files whose change is a change of generated output.
  static const outputs = [
    'packages/typed_openapi_tool/corpus.lock',
    'packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/goldens',
  ];

  static const _pubspec = 'packages/typed_openapi/pubspec.yaml';
  static const _changelog = 'packages/typed_openapi/CHANGELOG.md';

  @override
  String get name => 'semver';

  @override
  String get description =>
      'Check that a change of output has a CHANGELOG entry and a raised '
      'version.';

  @override
  Future<int> run() async {
    final results = argResults!;
    final root = results.option('root')!;
    final base = results.option('base')!;
    // The lock is compared from the first base that has one (M11 adds it);
    // the goldens always are.
    final lock = await _git(root, ['show', '$base:${outputs.first}']);
    final changed = await _git(root, [
      'diff',
      '--name-only',
      '$base...HEAD',
      '--',
      ...lock.isEmpty ? outputs.skip(1) : outputs,
    ]);
    final problems = const SemverRule().problems(
      outputChanged: changed.trim().isNotEmpty,
      basePubspec: await _git(root, ['show', '$base:$_pubspec']),
      pubspec: File('$root/$_pubspec').readAsStringSync(),
      baseChangelog: await _git(root, ['show', '$base:$_changelog']),
      changelog: File('$root/$_changelog').readAsStringSync(),
    );
    problems.forEach(_err.writeln);
    _out.writeln(
      changed.trim().isEmpty
          ? 'The generated output did not change.'
          : 'The generated output changed: ${problems.length} problems.',
    );
    return problems.isEmpty ? 0 : 1;
  }

  static Future<String> _git(String root, List<String> arguments) async {
    final result = await Process.run('git', ['-C', root, ...arguments]);
    return result.exitCode == 0 ? '${result.stdout}' : '';
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/lib/src/semver/semver_rule.dart`:

````dart
/// The rule of R15.4 (spec decision 7): a change of generated output for the
/// same input is a breaking change of the generator, so a change that
/// changes output must say so in the CHANGELOG and raise the version.
final class SemverRule {
  /// Creates the rule.
  const SemverRule();

  /// The heading of the CHANGELOG section that names changed output.
  static const String heading = '### Output changes';

  static final _version = RegExp(
    r'^version:\s*(\d+)\.(\d+)\.(\d+)',
    multiLine: true,
  );

  /// What the change must still add, one sentence each; empty when it
  /// complies. [outputChanged] says whether `corpus.lock` or a fixture's
  /// golden files changed; the pubspecs and CHANGELOGs are the generator's
  /// at the base and in the change.
  List<String> problems({
    required bool outputChanged,
    required String basePubspec,
    required String pubspec,
    required String baseChangelog,
    required String changelog,
  }) {
    if (!outputChanged) return const [];
    final base = _parse(basePubspec);
    final next = _parse(pubspec);
    final top = _topSection(changelog);
    return [
      if (changelog == baseChangelog || !top.contains('$heading\n- '))
        'The generated output changed (corpus.lock or a golden file): add '
            'an entry under "$heading" in the top section of '
            'packages/typed_openapi/CHANGELOG.md that names the clients and '
            'files affected.',
      if (base == null || next == null)
        'The version of packages/typed_openapi/pubspec.yaml cannot be read.'
      else if (!_raised(base, next))
        'The generated output changed: raise the version of '
            'packages/typed_openapi/pubspec.yaml from ${base.join('.')} to '
            '${_required(base)} or later (${base.first == 0 ? 'minor before '
                      '1.0' : 'major after 1.0'}).',
    ];
  }

  static List<int>? _parse(String pubspec) => switch (_version.firstMatch(
    pubspec,
  )) {
    final m? => [for (var i = 1; i <= 3; i++) int.parse(m[i] ?? '0')],
    null => null,
  };

  static bool _raised(List<int> base, List<int> next) => base.first == 0
      ? next.first > 0 || next[1] > base[1]
      : next.first > base.first;

  static String _required(List<int> base) =>
      base.first == 0 ? '0.${base[1] + 1}.0' : '${base.first + 1}.0.0';

  /// The text of the first `## ` section of [changelog].
  static String _topSection(String changelog) {
    final start = changelog.indexOf('## ');
    if (start < 0) return '';
    final end = changelog.indexOf('\n## ', start + 3);
    return changelog.substring(start, end < 0 ? changelog.length : end);
  }
}
````

- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: tasks 3, 4, 10, 11 (`corpus.dart check`, `corpus.dart semver`, `fuzz.dart`).
- Produces: the jobs `analyze-test` (ubuntu x `3.10.0`/stable, macOS and Windows with stable), `web` (dart2js, dart2wasm), `corpus` (`3.10.0`/stable), `fuzz` (200 seeds), `semver` (pull requests), `publish-dry-run`; nightly `beta` (`continue-on-error`), `corpus-windows`, `fuzz` (6000 seeds).

Spec decision 4. Format and analysis run once, on ubuntu with stable (decision 9 of this plan); every test suite, the tool's included, runs in every cell of the matrix. The corpus job caches the specs by the hash of `corpus.yaml` and runs `corpus check`, which does all four checks of the decision (generate and `--fatal-infos`, a second run writes nothing, the lock, `dart format --set-exit-if-changed` with that SDK's formatter on the output) and the round trips. The semver job needs the full history (`fetch-depth: 0`) to compare with `origin/$GITHUB_BASE_REF`. `test/ci/workflows_test.dart` pins the layers offline; `actionlint` validates the files. The cells this plan could not run (SDK 3.10.0, macOS, Windows, the nightly jobs) are verified only by the first CI run.

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/ci/workflows_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:yaml/yaml.dart';

/// The node at [path] of the workflow file [name] in `.github/workflows`.
Object? at(String name, List<Object> path) {
  final file = File(p.join('..', '..', '.github', 'workflows', name));
  Object? node = loadYaml(file.readAsStringSync());
  for (final key in path) {
    node = switch ((node, key)) {
      (final Map<Object?, Object?> map, _) => map[key],
      (final List<Object?> list, final int index) => list[index],
      _ => null,
    };
  }
  return node;
}

/// The `run` lines of the steps of the job [job] of the workflow [name].
List<String> runsOf(String name, String job) => [
  for (final step in at(name, ['jobs', job, 'steps']) as List<Object?>)
    if (step case {'run': final String run}) run,
];

void main() {
  test('every PR tests ubuntu with 3.10.0 and stable, macOS and Windows', () {
    const matrix = ['jobs', 'analyze-test', 'strategy', 'matrix'];
    expect(at('ci.yml', [...matrix, 'sdk']), ['3.10.0', 'stable']);
    expect(at('ci.yml', [...matrix, 'include', 0, 'os']), 'macos-latest');
    expect(at('ci.yml', [...matrix, 'include', 1, 'os']), 'windows-latest');
  });

  test('every PR runs the runtime on dart2js and dart2wasm', () {
    expect(at('ci.yml', ['jobs', 'web', 'strategy', 'matrix', 'compiler']), [
      'dart2js',
      'dart2wasm',
    ]);
  });

  test('every PR checks the corpus with both SDKs, the fuzz and semver', () {
    expect(at('ci.yml', ['jobs', 'corpus', 'strategy', 'matrix', 'sdk']), [
      '3.10.0',
      'stable',
    ]);
    expect(
      runsOf('ci.yml', 'corpus'),
      contains('dart run bin/corpus.dart check'),
    );
    expect(runsOf('ci.yml', 'fuzz'), contains(contains('--seeds 200')));
    expect(
      runsOf('ci.yml', 'semver'),
      contains(contains('corpus.dart semver')),
    );
  });

  test('nightly runs beta as a warning, the corpus on Windows, 6000 seeds', () {
    expect(at('nightly.yml', ['jobs', 'beta', 'continue-on-error']), isTrue);
    expect(
      at('nightly.yml', ['jobs', 'corpus-windows', 'runs-on']),
      'windows-latest',
    );
    expect(runsOf('nightly.yml', 'fuzz'), contains(contains('--seeds 6000')));
  });
}
````

**Create** `.github/workflows/nightly.yml`:

````yaml
name: Nightly

on:
  schedule:
    - cron: '0 3 * * *'
  workflow_dispatch:

# The nightly layer of the M11 matrix (spec decision 4): the beta SDK, where
# a failure is a warning only; the corpus on Windows; the long fuzz.
jobs:
  beta:
    name: Test (beta, warning only)
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    continue-on-error: true
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v5

      - uses: dart-lang/setup-dart@v1
        with:
          sdk: beta

      - name: Install dependencies (workspace)
        run: dart pub get

      - name: Test typed_openapi
        run: dart test
        working-directory: packages/typed_openapi

      - name: Test typed_openapi_runtime
        run: dart test
        working-directory: packages/typed_openapi_runtime

      - name: Test the runtime in Chrome
        run: dart test -p chrome
        working-directory: packages/typed_openapi_runtime

      - name: Check the corpus
        run: dart run bin/corpus.dart check
        working-directory: packages/typed_openapi_tool

      - name: Report a failure as a warning
        if: failure()
        run: echo "::warning::The beta SDK fails the suite; see the log."

  corpus-windows:
    name: Corpus (Windows)
    runs-on: windows-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v5

      - uses: dart-lang/setup-dart@v1
        with:
          sdk: stable

      - name: Install dependencies (workspace)
        run: dart pub get

      - name: Cache the corpus specs
        uses: actions/cache@v4
        with:
          path: packages/typed_openapi_tool/.dart_tool/corpus/specs
          key: corpus-specs-windows-${{ hashFiles('packages/typed_openapi_tool/corpus.yaml') }}

      - name: Check the corpus against corpus.lock
        run: dart run bin/corpus.dart check
        working-directory: packages/typed_openapi_tool

  fuzz:
    name: Escaping fuzz (6000 seeds)
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v5

      - uses: dart-lang/setup-dart@v1
        with:
          sdk: stable

      - name: Install dependencies (workspace)
        run: dart pub get

      - name: Fuzz
        run: dart run bin/fuzz.dart --seeds 6000
        working-directory: packages/typed_openapi_tool
````

### Task 13: The platform rows: `1.0` and NaN on the web, drive letters on Windows

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/diagnostics/display_paths_windows_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/test/platform/platform_semantics_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the runtime's `QueryParameter`, `DeepEquality`, `test/helpers/test_platform.dart`; the generator's `DisplayPaths`.
- Produces: tests only.

Spec decision 8, for the rows a test on a matrix platform can settle. `platform_semantics_test.dart` runs on the VM and, in the `web` job, on dart2js and dart2wasm, and pins each platform's behaviour: a `double` `1.0` in a query is `x=1.0` on the VM and Wasm and `x=1` in JavaScript (one number type), and `DeepEquality` finds `[NaN]` equal to `[NaN]` on the VM and Wasm but not in JavaScript (`identical` is `===` there; JSON has no NaN, so a decoded value never holds one). Both are documented limitations (task 9). `display_paths_windows_test.dart` (`@TestOn('windows')`, so only the Windows job runs it) pins `DisplayPaths` with drive letters: relative on the working directory's drive, also in another case, absolute with its letter on another drive; its expectations were checked with `package:path`'s Windows context on macOS. The case and hard-link tests of `generate` now run on Linux and Windows through the matrix (those that need `ln`, `kill` or POSIX modes already skip on Windows). SARIF is task 14, the browser's `Cookie` task 15; `style: cookie` stays a design decision ("Deferred").

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the tests**

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/diagnostics/display_paths_windows_test.dart`:

````dart
// Runs only on Windows, so only the Windows job of CI checks it (M11).
@TestOn('windows')
library;

import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diagnostics/display_paths.dart';

void main() {
  const paths = DisplayPaths(r'C:\work');

  test('a file on the drive of the working directory is relative', () {
    expect(paths.of(r'C:\work\specs\pets.yaml'), r'specs\pets.yaml');
    expect(paths.of(r'specs\pets.yaml'), r'specs\pets.yaml');
  });

  test('a file on another drive is absolute, with its drive letter', () {
    expect(paths.of(r'D:\specs\pets.yaml'), r'D:\specs\pets.yaml');
  });

  test('a drive letter in another case is the same drive', () {
    expect(paths.of(r'c:\work\pets.yaml'), 'pets.yaml');
  });
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/test/platform/platform_semantics_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_runtime/typed_openapi_runtime.dart';

import '../helpers/test_platform.dart';

/// What the runtime does differently on the VM, dart2js and dart2wasm (the
/// M11 matrix rows of the roadmap). Each test pins the behaviour of every
/// platform, so a change on one of them is seen; the differences are
/// documented limitations, not defects.
void main() {
  test('a double 1.0 in a query is 1.0, but 1 in JavaScript', () {
    // dart2js has one number type: 1.0 is the int 1 and prints as "1".
    expect(
      const QueryParameter('x', 1.0).encode(),
      TestPlatform.isJavaScript ? 'x=1' : 'x=1.0',
    );
    expect(const QueryParameter('x', 1.5).encode(), 'x=1.5');
  });

  test('NaN equals itself in DeepEquality, but not in JavaScript', () {
    // DeepEquality starts with identical(), which compares the bits of a
    // double on the VM and on Wasm and is === in JavaScript. JSON has no
    // NaN, so a decoded value never holds one.
    const equality = DeepEquality();
    expect(
      equality.equals([double.nan], [double.nan]),
      !TestPlatform.isJavaScript,
    );
    expect(equality.equals([1.5], [1.5]), isTrue);
  });
}
````

### Task 14: SARIF checked offline against its schema and GitHub code scanning; the reporter fixed

**Files:**
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/CHANGELOG.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/cli/check_command.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diagnostics/sarif_reporter.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/diagnostics/sarif_code_scanning_test.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/pubspec.yaml`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/fixtures/sarif/NOTICE.md`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/helpers/code_scanning_rules.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/helpers/json_schema.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/sarif/sarif_validity_test.dart`
- Download (by the step that says so): `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/fixtures/sarif/sarif-schema-2.1.0.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: M5 `SarifReporter`, `CheckCommand`, `ReportFormat`; the generator's public `DiagnosticBag`, `Diagnostic`, `RelatedLocation`, `DiagnosticCode`, `JsonPointer`, `Severity`.
- Produces: `const SarifReporter({String? defaultFile})`, `SarifReporter.fingerprintKey` (`typedOpenapi/v1`); tool test helpers `final class JsonSchema { const JsonSchema(Map<String, Object?> root); List<String> validate(Object? value); }` and `final class CodeScanningRules { const CodeScanningRules(); List<String> problems(Map<String, Object?> log); }`; `source_span` as a dev dependency of the tool.

Spec decision 8. The SARIF 2.1.0 JSON schema (Errata 01, OASIS) is vendored unchanged under the tool's `test/fixtures/sarif/` with a `NOTICE.md` (source, SHA-256, the OASIS copyright and IPR policy it is under); it is 112 KB, so the step downloads it and checks its hash rather than carrying it in this plan. `JsonSchema` checks the draft-04 subset the schema uses (`type`, `enum`, `properties`, `additionalProperties`, `required`, `items`, `minItems`, `uniqueItems`, `minimum`, `maximum`, `pattern`, `anyOf`, `oneOf`, local `$ref`), and `CodeScanningRules` the properties GitHub's "SARIF support for code scanning" marks required (and non-empty): `$schema`, `version`, `tool.driver.name`, a rule's `id`, `shortDescription.text`, `fullDescription.text` and `help.text`, a result's `message.text`, `locations[]`, `partialFingerprints`, and `physicalLocation.artifactLocation.uri` and `region.startLine`, `startColumn`, `endLine`, `endColumn`. The reporter's output was valid against the schema but broke GitHub's rules: no rule descriptions or help, no fingerprints, no region for a file without a position (which the M5 row asked about), and no physical location for a result without a file (`TOA901`, a missing config: GitHub rejects a whole upload with a result without a location). The reporter now writes the descriptions and help (from the code's id and slug), a partial fingerprint (the first 16 hex digits of the SHA-256 of code, file, pointer and message), a region on line 1 for a file without a position, and locates a result without a file at `defaultFile`, which `check --format sarif` sets to the config path. Programmatic use without `defaultFile` keeps such a result without a location (documented). No real upload to code scanning (out of scope).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/diagnostics/sarif_code_scanning_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:source_span/source_span.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diagnostics/diagnostic_bag.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diagnostics/json_pointer.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diagnostics/sarif_reporter.dart';

import '../helpers/cli_runner.dart';
import '../helpers/temp_dir.dart';

/// The single run of the SARIF log [text].
Map<String, Object?> runOf(String text) =>
    ((jsonDecode(text) as Map<String, Object?>)['runs']! as List<Object?>)
            .single!
        as Map<String, Object?>;

/// The results of [run].
List<Map<String, Object?>> resultsOf(Map<String, Object?> run) =>
    (run['results']! as List<Object?>).cast<Map<String, Object?>>();

/// The result of [results] whose message starts with [text].
Map<String, Object?> withMessage(
  List<Map<String, Object?>> results,
  String text,
) => results.firstWhere(
  (r) => ((r['message']! as Map)['text']! as String).startsWith(text),
);

/// The physical location of [result], or null.
Map<String, Object?>? physicalOf(Map<String, Object?> result) =>
    ((result['locations']! as List<Object?>).firstOrNull
            as Map<String, Object?>?)?['physicalLocation']
        as Map<String, Object?>?;

void main() {
  final source = SourceFile.fromString(
    'openapi: 3.0.3\npaths: {}\n',
    url: Uri.parse('api.yaml'),
  );
  DiagnosticBag sample() => DiagnosticBag()
    ..report(
      .binaryObject,
      'With a position.',
      file: 'api.yaml',
      pointer: JsonPointer.parse('#/paths'),
      span: source.span(15, 24),
    )
    ..report(.binaryObject, 'Without a position.', file: 'api.yaml')
    ..report(.configNotFound, 'Without a file.');

  test('every rule has the descriptions and help code scanning needs', () {
    final run = runOf(const SarifReporter().format(sample()));
    final driver =
        (run['tool']! as Map<String, Object?>)['driver']!
            as Map<String, Object?>;
    for (final rule in (driver['rules']! as List<Object?>).cast<Map>()) {
      for (final key in ['shortDescription', 'fullDescription', 'help']) {
        expect((rule[key] as Map)['text'], isNotEmpty, reason: key);
      }
    }
  });

  test('every result has a stable partial fingerprint', () {
    final first = resultsOf(runOf(const SarifReporter().format(sample())));
    final again = resultsOf(runOf(const SarifReporter().format(sample())));
    for (final (i, result) in first.indexed) {
      final prints = result['partialFingerprints']! as Map;
      expect(prints.values.single, matches(RegExp(r'^[0-9a-f]{16}$')));
      expect(again[i]['partialFingerprints'], prints);
    }
    expect(
      {for (final r in first) (r['partialFingerprints']! as Map).values.single},
      hasLength(3),
    );
  });

  test('a file without a position is located at its first line', () {
    final results = resultsOf(runOf(const SarifReporter().format(sample())));
    expect(physicalOf(withMessage(results, 'Without a position'))?['region'], {
      'startLine': 1,
      'startColumn': 1,
      'endLine': 1,
      'endColumn': 1,
    });
  });

  test('a result without a file is located at the default file', () {
    final located = resultsOf(
      runOf(
        const SarifReporter(defaultFile: 'typed_openapi.yaml').format(sample()),
      ),
    );
    expect(
      (physicalOf(withMessage(located, 'Without a file'))?['artifactLocation']
          as Map?)?['uri'],
      'typed_openapi.yaml',
    );
    final bare = resultsOf(runOf(const SarifReporter().format(sample())));
    expect(physicalOf(withMessage(bare, 'Without a file')), isNull);
  });

  test('check --format sarif locates a missing config at its path', () async {
    final temp = TempDir();
    final (code, out, _) = await CliRunner.run([
      'check',
      '--format',
      'sarif',
      '-c',
      'missing.yaml',
    ], workingDirectory: temp.path);
    expect(code, isNot(0));
    final result = resultsOf(runOf(out)).single;
    expect(result['ruleId'], 'TOA901');
    expect(
      (physicalOf(result)?['artifactLocation'] as Map?)?['uri'],
      'missing.yaml',
    );
  });
}
````

`sarif-schema-2.1.0.json` is the JSON schema of the Static Analysis Results

(SHA-256 `c3b4bb2d6093897483348925aaa73af03b3e3f4bd4ca38cef26dcb4212a2682e`).

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/helpers/code_scanning_rules.dart`:

````dart
/// The properties GitHub code scanning requires of an uploaded SARIF file
/// beyond its schema ("SARIF support for code scanning", the tables of
/// required properties): a driver name, a rule's descriptions and help, a
/// result's message, partial fingerprints and a physical location with a
/// region. An empty string does not count. Tests only.
final class CodeScanningRules {
  /// Creates the checker.
  const CodeScanningRules();

  /// The properties [log] lacks, each with its path; empty when it has all.
  List<String> problems(Map<String, Object?> log) => [
    ..._need(log, r'$schema', ''),
    ..._need(log, 'version', ''),
    for (final (i, run) in _list(log['runs']).indexed) ...[
      ..._need(run, 'tool.driver.name', '/runs/$i'),
      for (final (j, rule) in _list(_at(run, 'tool.driver.rules')).indexed)
        for (final path in [
          'id',
          'shortDescription.text',
          'fullDescription.text',
          'help.text',
        ])
          ..._need(rule, path, '/runs/$i/rules/$j'),
      for (final (j, result) in _list(run['results']).indexed)
        ..._result(result, '/runs/$i/results/$j'),
    ],
  ];

  List<String> _result(Map<String, Object?> result, String at) {
    final locations = _list(result['locations']);
    final prints = result['partialFingerprints'];
    return [
      ..._need(result, 'message.text', at),
      if (locations.isEmpty) '$at: no locations',
      if (prints is! Map || prints.isEmpty) '$at: no partialFingerprints',
      for (final (k, location) in locations.indexed)
        for (final path in [
          'physicalLocation.artifactLocation.uri',
          'physicalLocation.region.startLine',
          'physicalLocation.region.startColumn',
          'physicalLocation.region.endLine',
          'physicalLocation.region.endColumn',
        ])
          ..._need(location, path, '$at/locations/$k'),
    ];
  }

  static List<String> _need(Map<String, Object?> node, String path, String at) {
    final value = _at(node, path);
    return value == null || value == '' ? ['$at: $path is required'] : const [];
  }

  static Object? _at(Map<String, Object?> node, String path) {
    Object? current = node;
    for (final key in path.split('.')) {
      current = current is Map ? current[key] : null;
    }
    return current;
  }

  static List<Map<String, Object?>> _list(Object? value) => value is List
      ? value.whereType<Map<String, Object?>>().toList()
      : const [];
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/helpers/json_schema.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';

/// A validator for the part of JSON Schema draft-04 that the SARIF 2.1.0
/// schema uses: `type`, `enum`, `properties`, `additionalProperties`,
/// `required`, `items`, `minItems`, `uniqueItems`, `minimum`, `maximum`,
/// `pattern`, `anyOf`, `oneOf` and local `$ref`s (`#/definitions/...`).
/// `format`, `default` and the annotations are not checked. Tests only:
/// nothing ships it.
final class JsonSchema {
  /// Creates the validator of the schema [root].
  const JsonSchema(this.root);

  /// The schema, as decoded JSON.
  final Map<String, Object?> root;

  /// The problems of [value], each with its JSON pointer; empty when the
  /// value is valid.
  List<String> validate(Object? value) => _check(root, value, '');

  List<String> _check(Map<String, Object?> schema, Object? value, String at) {
    if (schema[r'$ref'] case final String ref) {
      return _check(_resolve(ref), value, at);
    }
    final problems = [
      if (schema['type'] case final Object type when !_hasType(value, type))
        '$at: expected $type, got ${jsonEncode(value)}',
      if (schema['enum'] case final List<Object?> values
          when !values.contains(value))
        '$at: ${jsonEncode(value)} is not one of $values',
      ..._number(schema, value, at),
      if (schema['pattern'] case final String pattern
          when value is String && !RegExp(pattern).hasMatch(value))
        '$at: "$value" does not match $pattern',
      ..._choices(schema, value, at),
    ];
    return [
      ...problems,
      if (value is Map<String, Object?>) ..._object(schema, value, at),
      if (value is List<Object?>) ..._array(schema, value, at),
    ];
  }

  Map<String, Object?> _resolve(String ref) {
    Object? node = root;
    for (final token in ref.substring(2).split('/')) {
      node = (node! as Map<String, Object?>)[token];
    }
    return node! as Map<String, Object?>;
  }

  static bool _hasType(Object? value, Object type) => switch (type) {
    final List<Object?> types => types.any((t) => _hasType(value, t!)),
    'object' => value is Map,
    'array' => value is List,
    'string' => value is String,
    'integer' => value is int || (value is double && value % 1 == 0),
    'number' => value is num,
    'boolean' => value is bool,
    'null' => value == null,
    _ => false,
  };

  static List<String> _number(
    Map<String, Object?> schema,
    Object? value,
    String at,
  ) => [
    if (value is num) ...[
      if (schema['minimum'] case final num min when value < min)
        '$at: $value is less than $min',
      if (schema['maximum'] case final num max when value > max)
        '$at: $value is more than $max',
    ],
  ];

  List<String> _choices(Map<String, Object?> schema, Object? value, String at) {
    final valid = <String, int>{
      for (final key in ['anyOf', 'oneOf'])
        if (schema[key] case final List<Object?> options)
          key: options
              .where(
                (o) => _check(o! as Map<String, Object?>, value, at).isEmpty,
              )
              .length,
    };
    return [
      if (valid['anyOf'] == 0) '$at: matches no schema of anyOf',
      if (valid['oneOf'] case final n? when n != 1)
        '$at: matches $n schemas of oneOf, not one',
    ];
  }

  List<String> _object(
    Map<String, Object?> schema,
    Map<String, Object?> value,
    String at,
  ) {
    final properties =
        schema['properties'] as Map<String, Object?>? ?? const {};
    final additional = schema['additionalProperties'];
    return [
      for (final key in schema['required'] as List<Object?>? ?? const [])
        if (!value.containsKey(key)) '$at: "$key" is required',
      for (final MapEntry(:key, value: item) in value.entries)
        ...switch (properties[key] ?? additional) {
          final Map<String, Object?> sub => _check(sub, item, '$at/$key'),
          false => ['$at: "$key" is not allowed'],
          _ => const <String>[],
        },
    ];
  }

  List<String> _array(
    Map<String, Object?> schema,
    List<Object?> value,
    String at,
  ) {
    final unique = value.map(jsonEncode).toSet().length == value.length;
    return [
      if (schema['minItems'] case final int min when value.length < min)
        '$at: fewer than $min items',
      if (schema['uniqueItems'] == true && !unique) '$at: items repeat',
      if (schema['items'] case final Map<String, Object?> items)
        for (final (i, item) in value.indexed) ..._check(items, item, '$at/$i'),
    ];
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/sarif/sarif_validity_test.dart`:

````dart
@TestOn('vm')
library;

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:source_span/source_span.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/typed_openapi.dart';

import '../helpers/code_scanning_rules.dart';
import '../helpers/json_schema.dart';

/// Every shape of result the reporter writes: a file with a position and a
/// fix, a related location, a file without a position, no file, and an
/// accepted (baselined) one.
DiagnosticBag sample() {
  final source = SourceFile.fromString(
    'openapi: 3.0.3\npaths: {}\n',
    url: Uri.parse('specs/my api.yaml'),
  );
  return DiagnosticBag()
    ..report(
      DiagnosticCode.binaryObject,
      'With a position.',
      file: 'specs/my api.yaml',
      pointer: JsonPointer.parse('#/paths'),
      span: source.span(15, 24),
      fix: 'Do something.',
    )
    ..add(
      Diagnostic(
        code: DiagnosticCode.overrideUnchanged,
        severity: Severity.info,
        message: 'With a related location.',
        file: '/abs/api.overrides.yaml',
        related: [
          RelatedLocation(
            message: 'The schema it changes.',
            file: 'specs/my api.yaml',
            pointer: JsonPointer.parse('#/paths'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    )
    ..report(DiagnosticCode.binaryObject, 'Without a position.', file: 'a.yaml')
    ..report(DiagnosticCode.configNotFound, 'Without a file.')
    ..addBaselined(
      const Diagnostic(
        code: DiagnosticCode.binaryObject,
        severity: Severity.warning,
        message: 'Accepted.',
        file: 'a.yaml',
      ),
    );
}

void main() {
  final schema = JsonSchema(
    jsonDecode(
          File(
            'test/fixtures/sarif/sarif-schema-2.1.0.json',
          ).readAsStringSync(),
        )
        as Map<String, Object?>,
  );
  Map<String, Object?> log(SarifReporter reporter) =>
      jsonDecode(reporter.format(sample(), showBaselined: true))
          as Map<String, Object?>;

  test('the SARIF output is valid against the SARIF 2.1.0 schema', () {
    expect(schema.validate(log(const SarifReporter())), isEmpty);
    expect(
      schema.validate(
        log(const SarifReporter(defaultFile: 'typed_openapi.yaml')),
      ),
      isEmpty,
    );
  });

  test('the SARIF output has what GitHub code scanning requires', () {
    const rules = CodeScanningRules();
    expect(
      rules.problems(
        log(const SarifReporter(defaultFile: 'typed_openapi.yaml')),
      ),
      isEmpty,
    );
    // Without a default file a result without one has no location.
    expect(rules.problems(log(const SarifReporter())), [
      '/runs/0/results/0: no locations',
    ]);
  });

  test('the validator finds what the schema forbids', () {
    final bad = log(const SarifReporter())
      ..remove('version')
      ..['extra'] = true;
    expect(schema.validate(bad), [
      contains('"version" is required'),
      contains('"extra" is not allowed'),
    ]);
  });
}
````


- [ ] **Step 2: Vendor the schema**

From the root:

```bash
curl -sfL https://docs.oasis-open.org/sarif/sarif/v2.1.0/errata01/os/schemas/sarif-schema-2.1.0.json -o packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/fixtures/sarif/sarif-schema-2.1.0.json
shasum -a 256 packages/typed_openapi_tool/test/fixtures/sarif/sarif-schema-2.1.0.json
```

Expected: `c3b4bb2d6093897483348925aaa73af03b3e3f4bd4ca38cef26dcb4212a2682e` (112,768 bytes).

- [ ] **Step 3: Run the tests to see them fail**

Run from `packages/typed_openapi`: `dart test test/diagnostics/sarif_code_scanning_test.dart`.
Expected: FAIL: `SarifReporter` has no `defaultFile`, rules have no descriptions, results no fingerprints (the tool's `test/sarif/sarif_validity_test.dart` fails on the same properties).

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the implementation**

### Task 15: The browser's `Cookie` header, checked in Chrome

**Files:**
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/README.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/README.ua.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/pubspec.yaml`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/test/dio/cookie_browser_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/test/dio/cookie_server.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the runtime's `DioRequestSender`, `OperationRequest`, `HeaderParameter`, `CookieParameter`; `package:test`'s `spawnHybridUri`.
- Produces: tests only; `stream_channel` as a dev dependency of the runtime.

Spec decision 8. `cookie_server.dart` is started in the VM by `spawnHybridUri` and listens on the loopback only (no external network): it answers every request with the `Cookie` and `X-Probe` headers it received and allows any origin (CORS, the preflight included). `cookie_browser_test.dart` (`@TestOn('browser')`) sends, from Chrome, a generated-style request with a header parameter and a cookie parameter, and a plain Dio request with both headers. Result on dart2js and dart2wasm: the `X-Probe` header arrives, the `Cookie` header never does (it is a forbidden request header for `XMLHttpRequest` and `fetch`). The claim of the runtime README is now checked; the README (both languages) and `CLAUDE.md` say so, and the roadmap row is closed (task 16). `style: cookie` (OpenAPI 3.2, unencoded cookie-octets) stays a design decision, parked. The port crosses the channel as JSON, which dart2wasm reads as a `double`, hence `num.toInt()`. `hybridMain` is the one top-level function in a test helper, as `package:test` requires.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the tests**

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/test/dio/cookie_browser_test.dart`:

````dart
/// Whether a `Cookie` header set through Dio reaches the server from a
/// browser (M11, spec decision 8): run with `dart test -p chrome` and
/// `--compiler dart2wasm`. The server is `cookie_server.dart`, started on
/// the loopback by `spawnHybridUri`.
@TestOn('browser')
library;

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_runtime/typed_openapi_runtime.dart';

void main() {
  late Dio dio;
  setUpAll(() async {
    final channel = spawnHybridUri('cookie_server.dart');
    // The port crosses as JSON: dart2wasm reads the number as a double.
    final port = (await channel.stream.first as num).toInt();
    dio = Dio(BaseOptions(baseUrl: 'http://localhost:$port'));
  });

  const probe = OperationInfo(
    operationId: 'probe',
    method: 'GET',
    pathTemplate: '/probe',
  );

  Result<Object?, Never> decode(RawResponse r) => switch (r.statusCode) {
    200 => r.success(r.json),
    _ => r.undocumented(),
  };

  test('the browser drops a Cookie header and sends the others', () async {
    final result = await DioRequestSender(dio).send(
      const OperationRequest(
        probe,
        headers: [HeaderParameter('X-Probe', 'sent')],
        cookies: [CookieParameter('session', 'abc')],
      ),
      decode,
    );
    final received = (result as Success<Object?, Never>).data! as Map;
    expect(received['probe'], 'sent');
    // `Cookie` is a forbidden request header: XMLHttpRequest and fetch
    // ignore it, so a cookie parameter never reaches the server.
    expect(received['cookie'], isNull);
  });

  test('a Cookie header set on Dio directly is dropped too', () async {
    final response = await dio.get<Map<String, Object?>>(
      '/probe',
      options: Options(headers: {'Cookie': 'session=abc', 'X-Probe': 'raw'}),
    );
    expect(response.data?['probe'], 'raw');
    expect(response.data?['cookie'], isNull);
  });
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/test/dio/cookie_server.dart`:

````dart
// A local HTTP server for `cookie_browser_test.dart`, started in the VM by
// `spawnHybridUri`: it answers every request with the `Cookie` and
// `X-Probe` headers it received, and allows any origin (CORS), so a page
// in Chrome can call it. It listens on the loopback only; no external
// network is used.
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:stream_channel/stream_channel.dart';

/// Starts the server and sends its port on [channel].
Future<void> hybridMain(StreamChannel<Object?> channel) async {
  final server = await HttpServer.bind(InternetAddress.loopbackIPv4, 0);
  server.listen((request) {
    final response = request.response;
    response.headers
      ..set(
        'Access-Control-Allow-Origin',
        request.headers.value('origin') ?? '*',
      )
      ..set('Access-Control-Allow-Credentials', 'true')
      ..set(
        'Access-Control-Allow-Headers',
        request.headers.value('access-control-request-headers') ?? '*',
      );
    if (request.method == 'OPTIONS') {
      response.close();
      return;
    }
    response
      ..headers.contentType = ContentType.json
      ..write(
        jsonEncode({
          'cookie': request.headers.value('cookie'),
          'probe': request.headers.value('x-probe'),
        }),
      )
      ..close();
  });
  channel.sink.add(server.port);
}
````

@@ -807,6 +814,57 @@ Rules of the pipeline:
   `78` the config file could not be loaded (TOA904 and per-client TOA902 exit
   `1`). Diagnostics a baseline accepts do not count.
 
+## Tools (`packages/typed_openapi_tool/`)
+
+The corpus, golden tests and fuzz of M11 (R15.3, R15.4); the tool uses the
+generator's public API only and is the only package with network access
+(`fetch`, never a test).
+
+- `corpus.yaml` pins each public spec (a URL fixed to a commit, a tag or a
+  release; `url` for one file, `archive` + `root` + `entry` for a GitHub
+  tarball of several), its SHA-256 (of the file, or `TreeHash` of the kept
+  tree), license and expectation (`generate` or `{check-errors: [...]}`).
+  `corpus.lock` is JSON with sorted keys: per `<union mode>/<client>` the
+  SHA-256 of every generated file (the manifest left out), the hash of the
+  whole output, the diagnostics by code, and the round-trip counts
+  (`examples`, `synthetic`, `spec_defects`, `failures`).
+- `lib/src/corpus/` - `CorpusListReader` (`corpus.yaml`), `SpecCache`
+  (downloads, verifies, `ArchiveExtractor` with the system `tar`),
+  `CorpusRun` (per mode a `CorpusPackage`: `GenerateRun` runs `generate`
+  twice through `TypedOpenApiCli` and `SpecChecker` once; the second run
+  must write nothing; `PackageGate` runs `dart analyze --fatal-infos` and
+  `dart format --set-exit-if-changed`; then a `RoundTrip` per client),
+  `CorpusLock`/`ClientLock`, and the commands `fetch`, `update`, `check`.
+  A round-trip failure does not fail `check` by itself: its count is in
+  the lock (0 for every client), so a new one is a difference.
+- `lib/src/roundtrip/` - `ExampleReader` (the `example`/`examples` of each
+  declaration's schema and of JSON responses, at the locations the IR
+  names), `SyntheticValues` (from the IR: required fields, all fields,
+  first and last enum value, every union variant with its discriminator
+  value), `RoundTripProgram` (a Dart program in the corpus package:
+  `fromJson`, `toJson`, JSON text, `fromJson`, `==`), `RoundTrip`.
+- `lib/src/fuzz/` - `FuzzSpec` (a spec and an overrides file per seed, with
+  `FuzzSchemas`, `FuzzOperations` and the `Hostile` strings), `FuzzBuild`
+  (six clients of one spec, in-process with `ApiLibrary`), `LeakCheck`
+  (no smuggled line, no raw control or bidi character in code or in the
+  text report), `LiteralCheck` (`DartLiterals` finds the literals of a
+  sampled output, the VM evaluates them, and every `SpecStrings` string of
+  the IR must be among them), `FuzzRun`.
+- `lib/src/semver/` - `SemverRule` and `corpus semver`: when `corpus.lock`
+  (once the base has one) or a golden file changed since the base, the
+  generator's CHANGELOG needs an entry under `### Output changes` in its
+  top section and the version a minor (before 1.0) or major raise.
+- `test/sarif/` - the SARIF output of `SarifReporter` against the SARIF
+  2.1.0 JSON schema, vendored in `test/fixtures/sarif/` with its OASIS
+  notice (`JsonSchema`, a test helper for the draft-04 subset the schema
+  uses), and against GitHub code scanning's required properties
+  (`CodeScanningRules`).
+- CI (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`, every PR): tests on ubuntu with 3.10.0
+  and stable, on macOS and Windows with stable; the runtime in Chrome with
+  dart2js and dart2wasm; the corpus with both SDKs; 200 fuzz seeds; semver.
+  `nightly.yml`: beta (a warning only), the corpus on Windows, 6000 seeds.
+  `test/ci/workflows_test.dart` pins these layers.
+
 ## Runtime (`packages/typed_openapi_runtime/lib/src/`)
 
 The only package generated clients depend on (dependencies: `dio`, `meta`, floor `^1.15.0` so Flutter's pinned `meta` resolves).
